Abstract: Perturbation theory for asymptotically stable systems has received sustained attention, leading to many criteria that preserve stability under uncertainty. A limitation of classical stability analysis, however, is that it is inherently equilibrium-dependent. Contractive systems, by contrast, are defined independently of any equilibrium and admit a rich set of robustness bounds. In this letter, we develop new time-varying perturbation conditions for contractive dynamics that preserve incremental exponential stability of the perturbed system and, in specific regimes, guarantee asymptotic convergence to solutions of the nominal dynamics. Abstract: Numerical computation of stochastic control problems under partial observation is challenging because the dynamic programming formulation is naturally posed on the conditional distribution of the hidden state. We propose particle-based methods that replace this infinite-dimensional filtering state by a finite-dimensional weighted particle system, building on recent limit theory for mean-field control with common-noise-adapted controls. We prove, under suitable assumptions, convergence of the fully discretized particle approximation to the original continuous-time partially observed control problem. The particle reformulation is high-dimensional but permutation-invariant, a structure that can be exploited by symmetric neural network architectures. We develop two deep learning algorithms: a direct optimization method for feedback controls and a Deep BSDE method for particle problems admitting a backward stochastic differential equation representation. We also extend the computational framework to partially observed mean-field control problems, which have been studied theoretically but remain less developed numerically. Numerical experiments on a linear--quadratic benchmark, a nonlinear partially observed mean-field control problem, and two financial applications, portfolio liquidation and asset allocation, demonstrate the accuracy and practical utility of the approach.

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of quantitative rapid stabilization via the linear quadratic (LQ) theory. Specifically, for non-self-adjoint parabolic equations, by virtue of the LQ theory, we derive the quantitative rapid stabilization by selecting a special cost functional and combining it with a quantitative observability inequality. Furthermore, this approach reveals the equivalence between the quantitative rapid stabilization and the quantitative observability inequality for linear systems. In addition, we apply this framework to the Navier--Stokes equations and establish the quantitative rapid stabilization around nontrivial steady states. Finally, we extend this methodology to finite-dimensional feedback laws in self-adjoint cases.

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel Adaptive Decentralized Quasi-Newton (AdaDQN) method for solving smooth nonconvex optimization problems over undirected networks. While existing decentralized algorithms with multiple local updates suffer from a pessimistic theoretical bottleneck, where the convergence stepsize must be inversely proportional to the number of local updates, our work overcomes this limitation. We revisit local update methods through a Majorization-Minimization (MM) lens and establish a Robust Inexact Algorithm (RIA) framework. Inspired by this framework, AdaDQN integrates a safeguarded consensus-aware termination criterion to dynamically balance local computational gains against consensus error, complemented by a scalable memoryless BFGS update and an event-triggered communication protocol to significantly reduce communication overhead. We establish the global convergence of AdaDQN to a first-order stationary point. Crucially, we prove that the convergence stepsize bound is decoupled from the reciprocal of maximum number of local updates. Numerical experiments demonstrate that AdaDQN achieves a superior computation-communication tradeoff, outperforming state-of-the-art decentralized methods across various performance metrics.

</details>

Abstract: Classical Euclidean instances for the Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (VRPTW) have floating-point arc costs derived from node coordinates. This raises reproducibility and numerical consistency issues. Hence, a common practice is to scale and round data to integer values, yet the impact of these choices is poorly understood. This paper studies the effect of integer scaling by a factor P and compares three rounding schemes with distinct feasibility and optimality guarantees. We analyze how scaling and rounding influence empirical performance of two well-known VRPTW solvers: Hybrid Genetic Search (HGS) and OR-Tools. We evaluate the quality of solutions mapped back to the original floating-point instances. Our results show that scaling and rounding can significantly affect both solver performance and solution quality. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for scaling and rounding in VRPTW benchmarks to improve efficiency, robustness and reproducibility.

</details>

Abstract: This paper investigates inverse problems for Linear-Quadratic-Gaussian (LQG) Mean Field Games (MFGs) based entirely on the observation of mean-covariance trajectories. We address three sequential challenges: identifying the optimal control for observed initializations, determining the control for arbitrary initializations, and recovering consistent cost parameters. After establishing the existence and uniqueness of the forward Nash equilibrium under mild hypotheses, we analyze the injectivity of the parameter-to-trajectory mapping, demonstrating that it is inherently non-injective and providing sufficient conditions for parameter equivalence. We prove that while the optimal control is locally identifiable for observed initializations under minimal assumptions, global identifiability requires a deeper structural recovery of the game's costs. To bridge this gap, we propose a constructive semidefinite programming method to infer cost parameters that are strictly consistent with the observed population dynamics. Numerical experiments illustrate this method.

Abstract: This letter presents a generalized technique for cancelling the parasitic inductance of capacitors using a lattice network. The Z-matrix-based equivalent transformation shows that the conventional vertically symmetric inductance condition is only a sufficient condition and can be relaxed. Abstract: In this paper we derive a framework for topological expansions of shape functionals with respect to multiple holes based on the expansion of one hole. Our strategy is similar to deriving a Taylor expansion of order two of functions in $\mathbf R^d$ by applying $d$-times a Taylor expansion in $\mathbf R$.
  For shape functionals defined on subsets of $\mathbf R^2$ we relate the topological expansion with multiple holes to a Taylor expansion with respect to the ball volumes where usual derivatives are replaced by topological state derivatives. The first topological state derivative was introduced in a previous paper and relates the first topological derivative of cost functionals. The second topological state derivative at two distinct points is introduced, similar to a second directional derivatives of functions defined on Banach spaces, as the topological state derivative of the topological state derivative. The second topological state derivative at the same point is defined as the "far-away" component of the compound layer expansion of the state variable.
  In dimension three the obtained topological expansion still involves first and second topological state derivatives, but it does not follow the same pattern of a Taylor expansion in the ball volumes. As we will show this dimension dependent behaviour is rather of geometrical nature and already appears in cost functionals without PDE constraints.

</details>

Motivation: 传统预测架构评估仅关注预测精度，但忽略了实际投资中偏好、交易成本和组合约束对模型可用性的影响。需要建立一个同时考虑这些实践因素的基准测试框架。

Result: 无模型在原始基准中占优，TransEnc-8最高rank-1可接受度仅0.352；排名随偏好、市场状态、特征空间和交易成本变化；在五模型约束组合比较中TransEnc-8始终被选，但所有模型在20bps成本下净夏普为负。

Conclusion: 该基准更适合模型选择与诊断工具，而非独立的交易策略声明；实际部署中需考虑多维度约束，纯预测精度评估不足以判断模型实用性。

Abstract: Benchmarking forecasting architectures for daily equity portfolios is not just a prediction exercise. It also asks which model remains usable after preferences, costs, and portfolio constraints are imposed. We build a CRSP daily-stock benchmark for 15 deep and statistical time-series architectures over 2018--2024. The protocol combines common-window decile portfolios, stochastic multi-criteria acceptability analysis, a deployment-adjusted acceptability index, and a constrained quadratic portfolio layer with capacity, beta, industry, risk, leverage, and turnover controls. The index starts from the SMAA rank-acceptability distribution and downweights models whose criteria-level wins produce high portfolio regret; its Gibbs form is characterized as an entropic update from the SMAA prior. Empirically, no architecture dominates the raw benchmark: TransEnc-8 has the largest rank-1 acceptability, 0.352, and no model exceeds about 0.36. Rankings vary with preferences, market state, feature universe, and transaction costs. In the promoted five-model constrained-portfolio comparison, TransEnc-8 is selected throughout, while return-oriented raw rankings can favor TS-RIDGE. Broad-universe decile signals can survive costs, but the baseline constrained-QP net Sharpe at 20 bps is negative for every promoted model. The benchmark supports model selection and diagnosis rather than a standalone trading-strategy claim.

</details>

Abstract: Riemannian optimization provides a powerful framework for constrained optimization by incorporating problem-specific structure directly into the geometry of the search space. In many applications, however, the explicit evaluation or application of the Riemannian metric can be computationally expensive or numerically unstable, limiting the practical efficiency of otherwise well-founded algorithms. Motivated by such settings, this work investigates to what extent classical Riemannian optimization algorithms can be reformulated without explicitly applying the metric. We show that many first-order components of Riemannian optimization only rely on the differential of the objective function and access to the Riemannian gradient, but not on explicit metric application. Based on this observation, we develop metric-free formulations and generalize optimization approaches to Finsler and Banach manifolds. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed metric-free strategies retain the effectiveness of their metric-dependent counterparts while significantly reducing computational overhead. These results highlight that a substantial portion of Riemannian optimization can be carried out independently of explicit metric application, broadening its applicability to problems with expensive or implicitly defined metrics.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究多项式微分变分不等式（PDVIs），提出了一种利用参数空间半代数分层结构的结构感知SOS-PDVIRK4算法，该算法结合四阶Runge-Kutta积分器与Moment-SOS层级，有效处理解的多重性和结构突变，并在约束网络-物理控制系统中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDVIs是一类重要的非光滑动力系统，其解的存在性、唯一性及数值求解面临挑战，尤其是参数VI解映射的间断结构和解的多重性问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 首先对参数多项式变分不等式进行分层分析，证明参数空间可划分为有限个互不相交的半代数区域，每个区域对应定性不同的解结构；然后证明PDVI在参数可行集中几乎处处存在最大正则解；最后提出结构感知SOS-PDVIRK4算法，该算法将四阶Runge-Kutta积分器与Moment-SOS层级相结合，并利用参数空间几何结构指导混合策略。

Result: 数值实验表明，在约束网络-物理控制系统中，相同初始状态但不同初始控制的轨迹存在显著差异，揭示了参数多项式VI解映射的间断结构；算法在数值收敛性研究中表现良好，并展示了探索PDVI系统长期行为的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构感知框架能够有效处理PDVI系统的解多重性和结构突变问题，为分析非光滑动力系统的长期行为提供了新的数值工具。

Abstract: This paper investigates Polynomial Differential Variational Inequalities (PDVIs), a class of non-smooth dynamical systems in which an ordinary differential equation is coupled with a time-dependent variational inequality (VI) defined by polynomials. As a foundation for polynomial differential variational inequalities, we investigate the parametric polynomial variational inequality and establish that, under mild assumptions, its parameter space admits a stratification into finitely many disjoint semi-algebraic regions, each corresponding to a qualitatively distinct solution structure. Furthermore, we establish the existence of maximal regular solutions to the PDVI for almost every initial condition in the parametric feasible set, where each solution has finitely many switches and evolves according to continuous semi-algebraic selection laws between switching times. Leveraging the structured partition of the parameter space, in which region boundaries correspond to discontinuities in the solution map of the parametric VI, we propose the structure-aware SOS-PDVIRK4 algorithm to handle solution multiplicity and structural transitions. This method integrates a fourth-order Runge--Kutta integrator with the Moment--SOS hierarchy and features a novel hybrid strategy that is explicitly guided by the underlying parameter space geometry. The efficacy of our structure-aware framework is demonstrated through numerical experiments on a constrained cyber-physical control system. Our numerical experiments demonstrate that, from identical initial states but different initial controls, trajectories can diverge significantly, revealing the discontinuous structure of the solution map of the parametric polynomial VI. The experiments also include numerical convergence studies and highlight the potential of our algorithm to explore the long-term behavior of PDVI systems.

</details>

Method: 采用欧洲空间显式优化模型，覆盖21种AI增长情景，系统量化数据中心的额外电力需求、装机容量需求、碳排放及运行影响。

Result: AI可驱动73-723 TWh额外需求，累计排放超调67-181 MtCO2；中等情景需额外200小时基荷发电，LCOE增加35 EUR/MWh；悲观情景需70 GW新增容量，管理增长路径下达226 GW；工作负载动态显著影响系统调度与排放。

Conclusion: 2050年净零目标可能实现，但中期碳排放风险显著；AI基础设施布局将由稳定电力和系统灵活性而非清洁能源丰裕度主导；政策需适应加速的数字转型以避免欧盟碳中和目标受损。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of AI globally has led to the proliferation of energy-intensive hyperscale data centres (DCs), making them as a structurally challenging component in power system planning and operation. Using a spatially explicit optimisation model of Europe across 21 AI growth scenarios, we systematically quantify additional demand, capacity requirements, emissions, and operational impacts of DCs. Results indicate that AI could drive 73-723 TWh of extra demand by 2050, risking cumulative emissions overshoots of 67-181 MtCO2 between 2030 and 2050. Our analysis indicates that after 2030, the geography of AI infrastructure will be shaped more by firm power and system flexibility than by the mere abundance of clean energy. In moderate scenarios, AI requires an additional of 200 hours of firm generation, which increases LCOE by 35 EUR/MWh in key hubs. We show that even under the pessimistic scenarios, existing infrastructure would require 70 GW additional capacity, while under managed growth pathways, this expansion could reach 226 GW. We further find DCs workload dynamics strongly shape energy dispatch, system flexibility, and emissions, while improved efficiency significantly reduces capacity needs, and system peaks. While our findings suggest that net-zero targets for 2050 may be achieved, critical emission risks may appear in the intermediate years, and the EU may compromise its carbon-neutral goals unless policies adapt to this accelerating digital transformation.

</details>

Method: 将原模型与具有自由停止（discretionary stopping）的最优控制平均场模型建立联系，通过特征化解的结构，并给出一个简单可显式求解的示例。进一步，对于具有兼容信息结构的N人博弈，证明从极限模型解出发可构造近似纳什均衡。

Result: 成功建立了原模型与自由停止最优控制平均场模型之间的联系，给出了解的特征化描述和显式可解实例，并证明了N人博弈中近似纳什均衡的存在性构造。

Conclusion: 本文拓展了平均场博弈理论，将隐藏状态和可选信息获取纳入框架，为更复杂的实际应用（如金融、经济、工程中的部分观测决策问题）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce a class of continuous time finite horizon mean field games where the objective function of the representative player depends on a hidden state, in addition to position, control, and the population distribution. While acting on the position dynamics, the agent has the option to pay for seeing the hidden state. We connect the original formulation of our model with a mean field model of optimal control with discretionary stopping, characterize solutions, and give a simple explicitly solvable example. For a class of $N$-player games with compatible information structure, we show that approximate Nash equilibria can be constructed starting from a solution to the limit model.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过分析归一化的N玩家α_N-势函数的最优值和极小化子的收敛性，建立与平均场控制(MFC)问题的联系；利用Wasserstein空间中的微分几何技术构造MFG的势函数；通过极限条件lim_{N→∞}α_N=0统一处理现有MFG势条件。

Result: 证明了归一化的N玩家α_N-势函数的最优值和极小化子收敛到带测度值控制的MFC问题的最优解；建立了lim_{N→∞}α_N=0与现有MFG势条件的等价性；证明了极限MFC问题的目标函数可作为对应MFG的势函数；作为副产品，建立了一般受控扩散（含共同噪声和非可分离控制交互）的混沌传播性质。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了有限玩家α-势博弈与平均场博弈之间的严格理论桥梁，提供了通过渐近条件lim_{N→∞}α_N=0从有限玩家博弈构造MFG势函数的新方法，为分析大规模动态博弈提供了统一框架。

Abstract: The framework of $α$-potential games has recently been introduced as a tool to analyze finite-player dynamic games, reducing the challenging task of finding approximate Nash equilibria to a control problem of minimizing a single function called $α$-potential function. In this work, we investigate the limiting behavior of $α$-potential games as the number of players $N$ tends to infinity. We show that potential mean field games (MFGs) arise naturally as this limit. Specifically, both the optimal values and the minimizers of normalized $N$-player $α_N$-potential functions converge to those of a mean field control (MFC) problem with measure-valued controls. We establish the equivalence of $\lim_{N\to\infty}α_N= 0$ with the existing conditions for potential MFGs, and provide an unified approach to construct the potential function for MFGs using the techniques from differential geometry in Wasserstein space. We further demonstrate that the objective of the limiting MFC problem serves as a potential function for the corresponding MFGs, an extension of the analogous finite-player setting. This connection yields new constructions of potential MFGs from a finite-player game, through the asymptotic condition $\lim_{N\to \infty}α_N= 0$. As a by-product, we establish propagation of chaos for $N$-player games converging to MFGs for general controlled diffusions with common noise and non-separable control interactions.

</details>

Method: 将[10,11]的曲面有限元框架扩展到基于弯曲Clough-Tocher三角剖分的Scott-Vogelius离散化；直接在细化曲面三角剖分上定义有限元空间，而非使用宏单元构造。

Abstract: We construct and analyze an exactly divergence-free Scott-Vogelius finite element method for the surface Stokes problem. The proposed scheme simultaneously enforces the tangentiality and incompressibility constructs exactly and has the same number of unknowns as the two-dimensional Euclidean discretization. Our construction extends the surface finite element framework of [10,11] to Scott--Vogelius discretizations defined on curved Clough--Tocher triangulations. In contrast to previous isoparametric Scott--Vogelius methods based on macro-element constructions, the present approach defines the finite element spaces directly on the refined surface triangulation, leading to a substantially simpler and more practical implementation. We prove inf-sup stability of the method and derive optimal-order convergence in the isoparametric regime.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有周期性散射问题的边界积分方程方法（如基于准周期格林函数的BIE、窗函数法WGF、以及直接PML-BIE方法）在Rayleigh-Wood异常附近均会出现数值发散或精度丧失的问题，亟需一种能够绕过RW异常障碍的鲁棒数值方法。

Result: 证明了PML截断边界积分算子的收敛性；通过多个数值算例验证了该方法在RW异常及其附近区域的高精度和鲁棒性能。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel frequency-robust perfectly matched layer (PML) boundary integral equation (BIE) method for solving two-dimensional electromagnetic scattering problems involving periodic arrays of obstacles. In periodic scattering problems, standard BIE formulations based on the quasi-periodic Green's function require the evaluation of lattice sums or challenging Sommerfeld-type integrals, which diverge at Rayleigh--Wood (RW) anomalies. An alternative is to use BIE formulations based on the Helmholtz free-space Green's function, but these are defined on unbounded unit-cell boundaries and therefore require suitable truncation strategies, such as the Windowed Green Function (WGF) method. Although such approaches avoid the use of expensive quasi-periodic Green's functions, they also suffer from breakdowns at RW anomalies unless an appropriate mode correction is incorporated. Similarly, the direct application of PML-BIE techniques to periodic structures experiences comparable difficulties near RW anomalies due to the destruction of exponential convergence near RW anomalies for fixed PML parameters. To overcome this challenge, we propose a modified PML-BIE method that combines the PML technique with a finite-mode correction, ensuring both high accuracy and robustness at and around RW-anomalies. Convergence of the PML-truncated boundary integral operators is proved and several numerical examples are presented to validate the efficiency and performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [98] [A Measure-Consistent Operator Learning Method for Infinite-Dimensional Master Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07976)
*Chenyao Wang,Hongyu Liu,Hui Liang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测度一致性算子学习方法（MCOL），用于求解平均场博弈论中的无穷维主方程。该方法通过经验测度表示种群分布，利用对称池化结构编码，并基于相同的粒子进行非局部残差的经验求积，同时引入内在导数与值函数的结构耦合近似，数值实验表明该方法在值函数、测度导数及反馈量的近似上具有高精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场博弈论中的主方程定义在概率测度空间上，涉及内在测度导数和非局部种群项，数值逼近极具挑战性，现有方法难以高效准确地求解。

Method: 用经验测度表示种群分布，通过对称池化结构将粒子信息编码为网络输入；利用相同粒子进行非局部残差的经验求积，避免额外积分网格；关键特点是残差中的内在导数由同一测度依赖表示诱导，形成值函数-导数的结构耦合近似；引入误差分解将神经逼近误差与经验离散误差分离。

Result: 多个主方程的数值实验表明，MCOL能够准确逼近值函数、内在测度导数及反馈量，并且在输入测度变化时保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MCOL为求解无穷维主方程提供了一种高效、鲁棒的算子学习方法，其测度一致性的设计思路具有理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: Master equations in mean field game theory characterize feedback value functions that depend on time, state (space), and the population distribution. Their numerical approximation is challenging because the unknown is defined on a space of probability measures and the equation involves intrinsic measure derivatives and nonlocal population terms. This paper proposes a measure-consistent operator learning method (MCOL) for infinite-dimensional master equations. The population distribution is represented by an empirical measure and encoded through a symmetric pooling structure, so that the network input is built directly from the particles representing the measure. The same particles are used in the empirical quadrature of the nonlocal residual terms, avoiding additional quadrature grids or auxiliary integration points. A key feature is that the intrinsic derivative appearing in the residual is induced by the same measure-dependent representation that defines the approximation of the value function. Consequently, the value function, its measure derivative, and the empirical residual are tied to a common measure representation, leading to a structurally coupled value-derivative approximation. We also introduce an error decomposition separating neural approximation error from empirical discretization error. Numerical experiments on several master equations show that MCOL accurately approximates the value function, intrinsic measure derivatives, and feedback quantities, and remains robust under changes in the input measures.

</details>


### [99] [A New Level Set Formulation for Improved Dirichlet Eigenvalue Minimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07979)
*Atharv Thakur*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文改进了基于水平集的Dirichlet特征值形状优化方法，包括重构水平集构建与求根流程、使用正则化近似目标函数，得到的计算优化器与已知最优解相当或更优，并讨论了若干开放子问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于水平集求解Dirichlet特征值形状优化问题的方法存在不足，需要改进其数值精度和计算效率，以逼近更优的几何构型。

Method: 对经典水平集构建和求根过程进行全面重构（overhaul），并引入正则化近似替代标准目标函数，在体积约束下求解特征值形状优化问题。

Result: 计算得到的最小化构型与文献中已知最优解相当或更优。

Conclusion: 该改进方法有效提升了Dirichlet特征值形状优化的计算质量，并为边界尖点存在性、p-参数化问题的特征函数权重末端行为、Pólya猜想相关的Weyl渐近性质等子问题提供了数值实验基础。

Abstract: This paper makes several improvements to existing level set based approaches to computing shape optimizers for the Dirichlet eigenvalues subject to a volume constraint. The most notable changes in formulation include an overhaul of the classical level set construction and root-finding procedures as well the use of a regularized approximation to the standard objective function. Our resulting computational minimizers are either comparable to or improvements on the best known minimizers from the literature. We conclude with a survey of subproblems within the field that may benefit from numerical experiments; these include the existence of cusps on the boundary, the end-behavior of eigenfunction weights in the p-parameterized problem, and the nature of Weyl asymptotics as they relate to the Pólya conjecture.

</details>


### [100] [Strain localization in softening plasticity without modifying standard constitutive models: a deformable Cosserat approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08095)
*Andrea Panteghini,M. B. Rubin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可变形Cosserat模型的应变局部化公式，通过严格分离耗散与能量机制，允许直接使用经典Cauchy连续体的标准弹塑性本构模型，无需修改应力更新算法或一致切线算子。微观连续体仅提供与方向场相关的线弹性项，自然引入内部长度尺度，控制局部化模式的演化。数值算例表明，在浅基础等问题中，网格细化下荷载-位移响应、耗散能和剪切带模式均收敛，验证了方法的鲁棒性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软化塑性模型中应变局部化问题的网格依赖性严重，现有Cosserat模型需要对本构模型进行修改，缺乏可直接利用经典Cauchy连续体标准弹塑性模型的通用框架。

Method: 采用可变形Cosserat模型，将耗散严格限制在宏观连续体，微观连续体仅贡献与方向场相关的线弹性项。无需修改应力更新算法或一致切线算子，在标准有限元框架中仅需添加额外的线性残差和切线算子贡献。

Result: 在浅基础问题中，采用Tresca和Matsuoka-Nakai塑性模型，包括高度不稳定的峰后响应，数值结果在网格细化下荷载-位移响应、耗散能和剪切带模式均收敛，即使存在非线性相互作用的局部化过程。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种鲁棒且物理一致的应变局部化分析途径，可在不修改本构模型的情况下处理软化塑性问题，具有较好的通用性和工程适用性。

Abstract: This paper presents a formulation for strain localization in softening plasticity based on a deformable Cosserat model. The approach enables the direct use of standard elastoplastic constitutive models formulated for a classical Cauchy continuum, without modifying the stress update algorithm or consistent tangent operator. A key feature of the framework is the strict separation of dissipative and energetic mechanisms: all dissipation is confined to the macro-continuum, while the micro-continuum contributes only through linear elastic terms associated with the director field. As a result, the constitutive structure of the elastoplastic model is preserved, and existing models can be employed as black-box components. The internal length scale arises naturally from the micro-continuum and governs the development, interaction and selection of localization patterns, rather than acting as a diffusive parameter. The formulation is easy to implement within standard finite element frameworks, requiring only additional linear contributions to the residual and tangent operators. The performance of the approach is assessed through benchmark problems involving shallow foundations on soil, a demanding test due to complex and unstable localization mechanisms. Both Tresca and Matsuoka-Nakai plasticity models are considered, including cases with highly unstable post-peak responses. Numerical results show convergence of load-displacement responses, dissipated energy and shear-band patterns upon mesh refinement, even in the presence of nonlinear interacting localization processes. These findings demonstrate a robust and physically consistent approach for the analysis of strain localization in softening plasticity.

</details>


### [101] [Quaternion Maximum-Volume Submatrix Selection with Applications to Multichannel Imaging and Visual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08175)
*Vsevolod Kliushev,Junjun Pan,Valentin Leplat*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出四元数矩阵的最大体积行/列选择方法（MaxVol和RectMaxVol），用于CUR低秩逼近和预条件处理，并应用于彩色图像、运动捕捉数据等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将基于最大体积原理的行列选择方法从实/复矩阵推广到四元数矩阵，克服四元数乘法非交换性带来的困难，为彩色图像、三维运动数据等多通道信号提供可解释且紧致的矩阵表示工具。

Method: 定义了四元数最大体积子矩阵（基于四元数奇异值和Study行列式）；推导了四元数秩一更新公式；提出两种选择过程：贪心方核方法（行列替换）和矩形方法（逐步扩展行集）；推导了附录行伪逆更新公式；建立了与拟优势条件的联系。

Result: 证明了行/列交换成功时四元数体积单调增加；推导了满秩情况下的精确四元数CUR恒等式和内插稳定性界；在矩形方法中得到了自然的右预条件子。实验验证了在RGB图像CUR逼近、病态四元数最小二乘预条件、运动捕捉数据行选择中的稳定性和有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的四元数MaxVol和RectMaxVol方法是稳定、高效的行列选择算法，能有效处理四元数数据中的低秩逼近和最小二乘问题。

Abstract: Low-rank approximation based on selected rows and columns is a useful alternative to singular value decompositions when the goal is an interpretable and compact matrix representation. A standard way to choose these rows and columns is the maximum-volume principle: it selects submatrices with large volume, which usually leads to stable interpolation coefficients and accurate CUR-type approximations. In this paper, we study this idea for quaternion matrices. This setting is natural for color images, three-dimensional motion data, and multi-channel signals, but requires care because quaternion multiplication is noncommutative. We define quaternion maximum-volume submatrix selection using quaternion singular values and the Study determinant. We then derive quaternion rank-one update formulas and use them to build two selection procedures: a greedy square-core method for row and column replacement, and a rectangular method that enlarges a selected row set until the interpolation coefficients are controlled. We prove that successful row and column swaps increase the quaternion volume of the selected square core when the exact quaternion inverse is used. We also connect the stopping criterion with quasi-dominance, prove an exact quaternion CUR identity in the full-rank case, and derive an interpolation stability bound. For the rectangular case, we derive an append-row pseudoinverse update and show how it gives a natural right preconditioner for overdetermined quaternion least-squares problems. Finally, we illustrate the methods on three applications: quaternion CUR approximation of RGB images, RectMaxVol-based preconditioning for ill-conditioned quaternion least-squares systems, and row selection in quaternion motion-capture data. The experiments show that the proposed quaternion MaxVol and RectMaxVol methods provide stable and efficient selection routines.

</details>


### [102] [Stable and Scalable Probabilistic Numerical Solvers for Stiff and High-Dimensional ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08203)
*Nathanael Bosch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对刚性和高维常微分方程问题，提出了两种互补策略：一是基于矩阵自由更新步骤（利用Jacobian-向量积、迭代线性求解器和随机协方差估计）实现线性扩展并保持稳定性；二是引入迭代重线性化将概率ODE求解器转化为全隐式方法，进一步提升稳定性。实验表明，所提方法在刚性和高维问题上相比已有概率求解器具有更好的稳定性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于滤波的概率ODE数值求解器在处理刚性和高维问题时存在两难：现有方法要么稳定但计算复杂度为O(d^3)，要么线性扩展但牺牲稳定性。本文旨在填补这一空白，开发既稳定又可扩展的概率ODE求解器。

Method: 提出两种互补策略：1) 矩阵自由更新步骤，使用Jacobian-向量积、迭代线性求解器和随机协方差估计实现线性复杂度，同时保持稳定性；2) 迭代重线性化，将概率ODE求解器转化为全隐式方法，进一步提升稳定性而不牺牲可扩展性。

Result: 在多个刚性和高维问题上的评估表明，所提方法在稳定性和可扩展性方面均优于已有的概率求解器。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了既稳定又可扩展的概率ODE求解器，解决了滤波类概率数值方法在刚性和高维场景下的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Filtering-based probabilistic numerical solvers for ordinary differential equations (ODEs) have been established as a flexible and efficient simulation framework with built-in numerical uncertainty quantification. However, problems that are both stiff and high-dimensional remain a challenge, as current methods are either stable and have cubic cost in the ODE dimension, or scale linearly at the expense of stability. In this paper, we close this gap and develop probabilistic ODE solvers that are both stable and scalable. We propose two complementary strategies. First, we develop a matrix-free update step that uses Jacobian-vector products, iterative linear solvers, and stochastic covariance estimation to enable linear scaling, all while retaining stability. Second, we propose iterative re-linearization to further improve stability without sacrificing scalability, turning probabilistic ODE solvers into fully implicit methods. We evaluate the proposed approaches on a range of stiff and high-dimensional problems and demonstrate improved stability and scalability over established probabilistic solvers.

</details>


### [103] [A Second-order Structure-preserving Parametric FEM for Surface Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08293)
*Beiping Duan,Zongze Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二阶时间精度、结构保持且网格鲁棒的参数化有限元方法，用于表面扩散和保体积平均曲率流。通过将原始演化方程重新表述为新系统，其中切向运动由调和映射热流控制，从而在离散设置中保持网格质量。体积保持性通过精心设计保证，能量耗散通过拉格朗日乘子实现。数值实验展示了二阶时间收敛性和网格质量保持优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理表面扩散和平均曲率流时，往往难以同时满足高阶时间精度、结构保持（体积守恒和能量耗散）以及网格质量鲁棒性。特别是曲面演化过程中网格退化和扭曲问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出二阶参数化有限元方法，核心创新在于将原始演化方程重新表述为调和映射热流控制切向运动的系统，使离散点沿切空间运动以降低调和能量；通过拉格朗日乘子强制执行能量耗散，并精心设计离散格式保证体积守恒。

Result: 数值实验验证了二阶时间收敛性、优异的网格质量保持能力（除非出现奇点），以及体积守恒和能量耗散等结构保持特性。

Conclusion: 该框架可推广至其他几何流问题，为高阶结构保持的曲面演化数值方法提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a second-order-in-time, structure-preserving, and mesh-robust parametric finite element method for surface diffusion and volume-preserving mean curvature flow. We first reformulate the original evolution equations into new systems in which the tangential motion is governed by a harmonic map heat flow. This heat flow maps a fixed reference surface onto the unknown evolving surface and drives points on the evolving surface to move in their tangent spaces so as to reduce the associated harmonic energy. As a result, in the discrete setting, the mesh quality can be maintained at a level comparable to that of the reference surface, unless singularities occur. The volume-preserving property is theoretically guaranteed by the careful design of the scheme, while energy dissipation is enforced through a Lagrange multiplier. We present several numerical experiments to demonstrate second-order convergence in time and the advantage of the proposed method in preserving mesh quality. The structure-preserving properties are further confirmed by the numerical results. Finally, the proposed framework can be readily extended to other geometric flows.

</details>


### [104] [Multiscale Fourier Neural Operator for Inverse Wave Scattering in Highly Oscillatory Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08448)
*Zilin You,Zhenli Xu,Wei Cai*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多尺度傅里叶神经算子（MscaleFNO）的算子学习方法，用于Helmholtz方程逆介质问题。MscaleFNO作为神经代理模型，将高频振荡介质映射到散射波场，并引入扩散模型进行正则化反演。数值实验证明该方法对高频介质的高精度重构能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Helmholtz方程逆介质问题中高频振荡介质难以精确重构的挑战，传统方法受限于谱偏差和正则化不足。

Method: 1) 提出MscaleFNO作为神经代理模型，降低谱偏差，将高频振荡介质映射到散射波场；2) 采用基于数据最小二乘的逆求解器；3) 引入即插即用的扩散模型进行正则化。

Result: 在二维高频介质的有限孔径反演数值实验中，MscaleFNO实现了对高频振荡介质属性的高精度重构。

Conclusion: MscaleFNO结合扩散模型正则化，在高频Helmholtz方程逆介质问题中具有显著优势，可实现高精度重构。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an operator learning method based on the multiscale Fourier neural operator (MscaleFNO) for inverse medium problems of Helmholtz equations. The MscaleFNO provides a neural surrogate model with reduced spectral bias for the Helmholtz equations, mapping highly oscillatory medium profiles to scattered wavefields. A plug-and-play inversion using elucidated diffusion model is introduced to regularize the inverse solver based on least squares of data misfits. Numerical results for partial aperture inversion of oscillatory two-dimensional media demonstrate the advantage and effectiveness of MscaleFNO for accurate reconstruction of highly oscillatory medium properties.

</details>


### [105] [Stable Triangle Projections for Variable-Degree Tetrahedral Spaces and Uniform IPDG Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08516)
*Situan Li,Weiying Zheng*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在三角形上局部变自由度、且在L2和H1范数下均一致稳定的投影算子，并将其用于两种四面体有限元构造：一是用于构建H1稳定的高阶到变自由度投影，进而得到hp空间的一致稳定分解和加性Schwarz预条件子；二是用于在一致正则映射网格上实现DG-to-CG稳定分解，从而得到对称IPDG范数下的预条件子。所有常数仅依赖于参考形状、局部自由度跨度、相邻自由度、映射正则性、补丁基数及系数路径常数，与h、局部多项式阶数和系数对比度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的hp有限元预条件子通常在h或p方向上存在依赖性，或对变自由度网格缺乏稳定性保证。本文旨在构建一种在h和p上均一致稳定、适用于变自由度hp空间（包括协调和非协调DG格式）的预条件子框架。

Method: 1）设计一个在三角形上、针对边变自由度的局部投影算子，在L2和H1范数下一致稳定；2）将该算子用于四面体参考单元，构建从高阶多项式空间到变自由度空间的H1稳定投影，该投影局部且迹兼容；3）利用该投影得到协调hp空间的h/p一致稳定分解及加性Schwarz预条件子；4）在一致正则映射网格上，利用同一三角形投影实现有限层边截断，得到对称IPDG范数下p一致稳定的DG-to-CG分解，进而得到IPDG预条件子。

Result: 成功构建了在L2和H1范数下均匀稳定的三角形变自由度投影算子，并基于此得到了两种四面体上的预条件子：一是协调Laplace算子的加性Schwarz预条件子，二是对称IPDG范数下的IPDG预条件子。收敛常数与h、局部多项式阶数和系数对比度无关。

Conclusion: 所提出的三角形投影算子是构建变自由度hp空间一致稳定分解的关键工具，其局部性和迹兼容性使得协调和DG两种格式的预条件子均能达到h、p和系数无关的收敛性，极大提升了hp有限元预处理器的鲁棒性和适用性。

Abstract: The main ingredient of this paper is an edge-local variable-degree projection on a triangle that is uniformly stable in both L2 and H1. We use this two-dimensional operator in two tetrahedral constructions. First, on a reference tetrahedron, we build an H1-stable projection from a high order polynomial space onto a variable-degree space whose degrees are prescribed independently on edges, faces, and in the volume. Since the tetrahedral projection is local and trace-compatible, it also gives an h- and p-uniform stable decomposition, in the weighted energy norm, for conforming hp spaces, and hence a uniform additive Schwarz preconditioner for the conforming Laplace operator. Second, on a uniformly regular mapped tetrahedral mesh with elementwise variable polynomial degrees, the same triangular projection gives the finite-layer edge truncation needed in a p-uniform stable DG-to-CG decomposition for the symmetric IPDG norm. The DG-to-CG decomposition, combined with the conforming splitting, gives the IPDG preconditioner. The constants depend only on reference shapes, the local degree-spread bound within each tetrahedron, the neighbor-degree bound across mesh faces, uniform map-regularity, patch cardinalities, and the coefficient path constants; they are independent of h, of the local polynomial degrees, and of the coefficient contrast.

</details>


### [106] [A Mixed Extended Virtual Element Method for Elliptic Interface Problems on Polygonal Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08526)
*Xianyan Zheng,Jinru Chen,Feng Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于椭圆界面问题的低阶H(div)协调混合扩展虚拟元方法，在非拟合多边形网格上实现了通量和压力的最优一阶误差估计，且常数独立于网格尺寸和界面位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决椭圆界面问题中，界面非拟合多边形网格上的通量和压力逼近，特别是处理切割单元上非多项式成分带来的额外一致性项。

Method: 采用子域扩展H(div)-VEM空间逼近通量、分片常数逼近压力；在切割单元上定义基于整个背景单元的多项式投影并限制到两个子域；使用增强核稳定化和界面法向通量平均处理切割单元的一致性项；添加修正界面通量罚项和局部发散幽灵罚项实现切割位置无关的稳定性。

Result: 证明了连续性、离散inf-sup条件以及网格依赖范数下的最优一阶误差估计；常数独立于网格尺寸和界面相对于背景网格的位置。

Conclusion: 该方法在非拟合网格上有效求解椭圆界面问题，误差估计的常数独立于界面位置，但可能依赖于系数对比度。

Abstract: We propose a lowest-order \(H(\operatorname{div})\)-conforming mixed extended virtual element method for elliptic interface problems on interface-unfitted polygonal meshes. The flux and pressure are approximated by subdomain-wise extended \(H(\operatorname{div})\)-VEM spaces and by piecewise constants, respectively. On cut elements, the computable polynomial projection is defined on the whole background element and then restricted to the two subdomains. Compared with BDM-type polynomial spaces, the mixed VEM space contains a non-polynomial component, which gives rise to additional consistency terms on cut elements. To control these terms, we use an enhanced kernel stabilization on cut elements and an interface normal-flux average in the mixed coupling. A corrected interface-flux penalty and a local divergence ghost penalty are added to obtain cut-position-independent stability without using a volume div-div augmentation. We prove continuity, a discrete inf-sup condition, and an optimal first-order error estimate in a mesh-dependent norm. The constants are independent of the mesh size and of the position of the interface relative to the background mesh, but may depend on the coefficient contrast.

</details>


### [107] [Novel physical property preserved methods for stochastic Schrödinger--KdV equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08585)
*Ziheng Chen,Jialin Hong,Liying Sun*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文从分析和数值两个角度研究了加性噪声驱动的随机薛定谔-科尔特韦格-德弗里斯方程，提出了两种保结构的时空离散格式，分别基于分裂-Crank-Nicolson方法和常数标量辅助变量框架，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立随机薛定谔-KdV方程的物理量演化规律，并基于这些内在结构设计能够保持离散物理定律的数值格式。

Method: 首先推导平均等离子体数、动量和能量的演化律及平均粒子数守恒律。基于分裂策略和Crank-Nicolson格式构造第一种时间离散，保持离散平均等离子体数、动量和粒子数的演化/守恒律；采用常数标量辅助变量框架构造第二种时间离散，保持修正的平均能量律。结合局部间断Galerkin空间离散得到保结构全离散格式。

Result: 两种时间离散格式分别保持了相应的离散物理定律（平均等离子体数、动量、粒子数守恒/演化律，以及修正的平均能量律），结合LDG空间离散的全离散格式继承了这些保结构性质，数值实验验证了精度、鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的保结构数值方法能够准确模拟随机薛定谔-KdV方程，同时保持重要的物理不变量，为随机非线性波动方程的数值求解提供了有效途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study the stochastic Schrödinger--KdV equation driven by additive noise from both analytical and numerical viewpoints. We first establish the evolution laws for the averaged plasmon number, momentum, and energy, together with the conservation of the averaged particle number. Motivated by these intrinsic structures, we develop two temporal discretizations. One is constructed based on the splitting strategy and Crank--Nicolson scheme, and is shown to preserve the discrete evolution laws of the averaged plasmon number and momentum, as well as the discrete conservation law of the averaged particle number. The other is proposed within the constant scalar auxiliary variable framework, in which the nonlinear energy functional is reformulated so that a modified averaged energy law can be preserved at the discrete level. Combining these temporal discretizations with a local discontinuous Galerkin approximation in space yields structure-preserving full discretizations inheriting the corresponding discrete physical laws. Numerical experiments are presented to validate the theoretical results and to demonstrate the accuracy, robustness, and effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [108] [ND-TNN: Tensor-Neural-Network Approximation for High-Dimensional Nonlocal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08685)
*Ziyue Cai,Zuoqiang Shi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于张量神经网络（TNN）架构求解高维非局部扩散模型的数值方法，利用TNN的张量积结构与高斯核的可分离性，将高维非局部能量积分分解为低维积分乘积，并建立了Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件下的误差估计理论，通过多达20维的张量积区域及L形区域数值实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维非局部扩散模型的数值求解面临维度灾难的挑战，传统数值方法在高维空间中计算成本极高。TNN架构为突破这一瓶颈提供了可能，但尚未有将其应用于非局部模型的理论分析与数值验证。

Method: 基于TNN架构，利用其张量积结构将高维非局部能量积分转化为低维积分（通过Gauss-Legendre求积计算）；对不可分离的源项和边界数据采用TNN预处理步骤处理；分别针对Dirichlet和Neumann边界条件建立误差估计理论。

Result: 对于Dirichlet边界条件，建立了渐近相容的L²误差估计；对于Neumann边界条件，L²估计得到改进，并通过平滑后处理步骤进一步获得H¹梯度估计。在最高20维的张量积区域上验证了理论结果，并在2D和3D L形区域上证明了方法的实际鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TNN方法能够有效求解高维非局部扩散模型，具有理论上的误差保证和实际中的数值鲁棒性，且能处理非光滑域情况。

Abstract: We study a numerical method, built on the tensor neural network (TNN) architecture introduced in \cite{wang2022tensor}, for solving nonlocal diffusion models in high-dimensional spaces. The tensor-product structure of the TNN ansatz, combined with the separability of the Gaussian kernel, reduces the high-dimensional integrals in the nonlocal energy to products of low-dimensional integrals, which are evaluated by Gauss--Legendre quadrature; nonseparable source and boundary data are handled by a TNN-based preconditioning step. For the Dirichlet boundary condition, we establish the asymptotically compatible $L^2$ error estimate \[ \|u_{\mathrm{loc}}-u_{δ,p}\|_{L^2(Ω)} \le C\!\left(\frac{\varepsilon_f}{\sqrtδ} +\frac{\varepsilon_g}δ +\frac{\varepsilon_u}{\sqrtδ} +η_{\mathrm{opt}}\right) +C\sqrtδ, \] where $\varepsilon_f$, $\varepsilon_g$ and $\varepsilon_u$ are the data and trial-class approximation errors and $η_{\mathrm{opt}}$ is the optimization residual. For the Neumann boundary condition, the $L^2$ estimate is improved to $O(\varepsilon_f+\varepsilon_g/\sqrtδ+\varepsilon_u +η_{\mathrm{opt}}+δ)$, and an $H^1$ gradient estimate is further obtained through a smoothing post-processing step. Numerical experiments on tensor-product domains up to $d=20$ support the theoretical results, and additional tests on two- and three-dimensional $L$-shaped domains demonstrate the practical robustness of the method beyond the smooth-domain setting covered by the analysis.

</details>


### [109] [Simultaneous recovery of multiple parameters in nonlocal diffusion equations from internal measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08699)
*Kai Yu,Zhiyuan Li,Yikan Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对非局部扩散方程，从内部测量数据中同时反演多个参数，证明了唯一性，并利用Levenberg-Marquardt方法实现了稳定的数值重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从内部测量数据中同时恢复非局部扩散方程的多个参数，解决该逆问题的唯一性与数值重建难题。

Method: 理论部分利用解的渐近行为、解析延拓、拉普拉斯变换及解析函数性质证明唯一性；数值部分采用Levenberg-Marquardt方法求解逆问题。

Result: 数值算例验证了所提算法的有效性和理论结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文为非局部扩散方程的多参数同时反演提供了理论唯一性保证和稳定的数值求解方案。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to simultaneously recovering multiple parameters from internal measurements for nonlocal diffusion equations. The uniqueness of the inverse problem is established by employing the asymptotic behavior of solutions, analytic continuation, the Laplace transform, and properties of analytic functions. For numerical reconstruction, we apply the Levenberg-Marquardt method to obtain a stable approximate solution of the inverse problem. Numerical examples are provided to demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed algorithm and to validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [110] [Compositional Approximation Can Strictly Outperform Superpositional Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08727)
*Dennis Elbrächter,Philipp Petersen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了某些函数类在叠加方法（如线性组合字典元素）与组合方法（如神经网络）下的逼近速率差异，发现存在结构特性使叠加方法的逼近速率严格低于组合方法，且两者间差距可以任意大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典研究中许多函数类被叠加方法（如字典线性组合）最优逼近，而组合方法（如神经网络）结构不同。本文旨在探究是否存在某些函数类，其结构特性导致叠加方法的逼近速率本质低于组合方法，从而揭示两类方法在表达能力上的根本差异。

Method: 通过构造显式示例，分析满足特定结构性质的函数类，对比叠加方法与组合方法（如神经网络）在均匀逼近误差随参数数量变化的衰减速率，并在保证组合方法参数可编码为比例比特串的前提下进行比较。

Result: 构造出具体的函数类示例，其中叠加方法的逼近速率严格低于组合方法的逼近速率，且两者之间的差距可以任意大，即存在任意大的逼近速率鸿沟。

Conclusion: 存在结构特殊的函数类，其叠加逼近速率本质受限，而组合方法（如神经网络）可以取得更优的逼近效果，两类方法的逼近能力存在根本性不对称。

Abstract: Many classically studied function classes are known to be approximated optimally by superpositional methods, i.e. with approximants constructed as the linear combination of elements in some dictionary. Here optimality means that the uniform approximation error viewed as a function of the number of parameters used has polynomial decay of the highest order achievable by any parametrized method whose parameters can be encoded as a bit string of length proportional, up to logarithmic factors, to the number of parameters. While compositional methods like neural networks are structurally different, their approximation rates can be made comparable by imposing constraints that ensure such a proportional bit string encoding. In this work we study function classes exhibiting structural properties that limit superpositional approximation rates to be strictly lower than compositional approximation rates. In particular, we construct explicit examples for which there is an arbitrarily large gap.

</details>


### [111] [Low-Variance Randomised Numerical Linear Algebra for Finite Element Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08817)
*N. Polydorides,Y. Wu,. H. Noori,H. Vandierendonck,R. Woods*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种低方差随机数值线性代数方法，用于参数化椭圆偏微分方程的多查询有限元系统，结合Galerkin子空间投影、参数无关杠杆得分伯努利采样和控制变量方案，通过融合正向和逆向草图构建高效正则化估计器，在保持有限元公式结构、稳定性和精度的同时降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多查询有限元系统在数字孪生和在线模型校准等应用中计算成本过高的问题，特别是参数化椭圆偏微分方程场景下需要高效降低维度并减少方差的需求。

Method: 采用Galerkin子空间投影进行降维，结合参数无关杠杆得分伯努利采样与控制变量方案，生成低方差的正向草图和可逆的逆向草图，并将两者融合为单一正则化估计器。

Result: 当参数场变化不剧烈时，控制变量与草图融合的协同作用可大幅补偿次优参数无关采样带来的损失，在时间、内存和通信方面实现显著节省，同时保持科学模拟可接受的精度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效降低了多查询有限元系统的计算成本，在参数场平滑变化时表现优异，适用于数字孪生和在线模型校准等对效率要求高的应用场景。

Abstract: We present a low-variance randomised numerical linear algebra approach for multi-query finite element systems arising from parametric elliptic partial differential equations with applications to digital twins and online model calibration. The method relies on Galerkin subspace projection for reducing the dimensionality, and then combines parameter-oblivious leverage-score Bernoulli sampling with a control variates scheme to yield a reduced-variance `forward' sketch and an invertible `inverse' sketch that are then fused to a single efficient regularised estimator. Effectively, this reduces the computational cost in computing the projected system of equations while preserving the structure, stability, and accuracy of the underlying FEM formulation. We derive probabilistic bounds for the sketching error, invertibility, and estimator variance, and then validate the method on large-scale example problems. The results show that when the parameter fields do not vary too sharply, the synergy of control variates together with the sketch fusion can largely offset the loss incurred by the sub-optimal parameter-oblivious sampling. In this regime, our method achieves substantial savings in time, memory, and communication while maintaining accuracy levels that are acceptable for scientific simulation.

</details>


### [112] [Fourier Neural Operators with rank-1 lattice points and hyperbolic cross](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08871)
*Jakob Dilen,Alexander Keller,Frances Y. Kuo,Dirk Nuyens*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出基于秩1格点和双曲交叉频率指标集的晶格傅里叶神经算子（FNO），通过用精心构造的秩1格点替代空间张量积网格、在参数空间使用第二组格点作为训练点，在减少网络参数、空间点和训练样本的同时提升了逼近精度和效率，并简化了架构（仅需一维FFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准FNO基于多维傅里叶变换在张量积网格上实现，存在计算效率低、参数冗余、泛化误差受限于离散化方式等问题。作者旨在通过更好的离散化策略（格点理论和双曲交叉频率指标集）来提升FNO的泛化性能和计算效率。

Method: 1) 推导FNO关于空间变量和参数变量的一般正则性界；2) 用精心构造的秩1格点替代空间张量积网格；3) 在参数空间中用第二组格点作为训练点；4) 采用双曲交叉频率指标集代替全张量积频率集；5) 将高维傅里叶变换简化为只需一维快速傅里叶变换（FFT）。

Result: 在更少的网络参数、更少的空间点和更少的训练样本条件下，实现了更精确高效的逼近；架构因使用秩1格点而简化（仅需一维FFT）。

Conclusion: 所提出的格点基双曲交叉FNO（lattice-based hyperbolic-cross FNOs）在环面上的椭圆偏微分方程求解中展现出优势，验证了通过优化离散化策略可同时提升FNO的泛化误差、计算效率和架构简洁性。

Abstract: The \emph{Fourier neural operator} (FNO) is a neural network architecture that learns mappings between function spaces. Its efficient implementation is based on the multi-dimensional Fourier transform. By deriving general regularity bounds for the FNO with respect to both the spatial and parametric variables, we prove that the generalization error of the FNO can be improved by replacing spatial tensor product grids with purpose-built rank-1 lattice points, and by using a second lattice carefully constructed as training points in the parametric space. We achieve more accurate and efficient approximations from fewer network parameters, fewer spatial points, and fewer training samples. In addition, the architecture is simplified, because the high-dimensional Fourier transform on rank-1 lattices requires only a \emph{one-dimensional fast Fourier transform}, and we can use a \emph{hyperbolic cross} frequency index set with lattice points. We demonstrate the benefits of our \emph{lattice-based hyperbolic-cross FNOs} for an elliptic PDE on the torus.

</details>


### [113] [Accelerating GMRES with Matrix-Free Multiscale Robin Preconditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08883)
*Dilong Zhou,Rafael T. Guiraldello,Felipe Pereira,Fabrício S. Sousa*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多重尺度Robin耦合过采样方法（MRCM-OS）的无矩阵右预处理策略，用于GMRES求解地下流体椭圆问题。通过局部子域求解和过采样平滑构建预处理器，无需显式组装全局算子。数值实验表明，该方法在多种高对比度地下流动问题中显著减少了迭代次数，结合过采样和平滑后常在一到两次GMRES迭代内收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对地下流体流动中的椭圆问题，传统GMRES方法在处理高对比度、多尺度问题时收敛缓慢，需要高效的预处理策略来加速迭代求解过程。

Method: 提出了基于多重尺度Robin耦合方法且带过采样（MRCM-OS）的无矩阵右预处理策略，通过局部子域求解和过采样平滑构建预处理器，无需显式组装全局算子，应用于GMRES方法中。

Result: 数值实验表明，在多种挑战性高对比度地下流动问题中，该方法显著减少了GMRES迭代次数；采用过采样和平滑后，许多情形下仅需一到两次迭代即可收敛。

Conclusion: 将GMRES与多重尺度Robin算子相结合是构建快速收敛预处理策略的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: We propose a matrix-free right-preconditioning strategy for the Generalized Minimal Residual (GMRES) method based on the Multiscale Robin Coupled Method with oversampling (MRCM-OS) for the numerical solution of elliptic problems arising in subsurface flow. The resulting preconditioner is constructed through local subdomain solves with oversampling and smoothing, and can be applied without explicit assembly of the global operator.
  After a careful presentation of the new procedure, it is used in extensive numerical experiments. Our results demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially reduces iteration counts across a range of challenging, high-contrast subsurface flow problems. In many cases, convergence is obtained in one or two GMRES iterations when oversampling and smoothing are employed.
  The results indicate that combining GMRES with multiscale Robin-based operators is a promising direction for the construction of rapidly convergent preconditioning strategies.

</details>


### [114] [High-Order Regularity and a Fully Discrete Fourier Spectral Method for a Partially Dissipative Viscoelastic Timoshenko System with Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09007)
*Zhenyang Zhong,Hui Liang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类由记忆项驱动的部分耗散粘弹性Timoshenko系统，建立了有限时间区间上的适定性理论，构造了时间和空间分别具有二阶和q阶收敛的Fourier谱全离散格式，并通过数值实验验证了理论收敛率及记忆项在能量耗散和振动衰减中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由Volterra型记忆项驱动的部分耗散粘弹性Timoshenko系统的适定性及高效数值逼近方法，以准确描述粘弹性材料中的能量耗散和振动衰减行为。

Method: 理论分析方面，建立弱解和强解的存在唯一性、稳定性及高阶正则性；数值方法方面，构造Fourier谱全离散格式：空间上用正弦/余弦基展开处理Dirichlet/Neumann边界条件，时间上采用中心差分处理二阶导数，复合梯形公式逼近记忆卷积项；运用离散能量法证明离散能量的正定性，并建立误差估计。

Result: 建立了全离散格式在时间上二阶收敛、空间上q阶收敛（任意q∈ℕ）的误差估计；数值实验验证了理论收敛率，并对比了局部与非局部模型的动态响应，表明记忆项能有效刻画粘弹性材料的能量耗散和振动衰减。

Conclusion: 记忆效应对粘弹性材料的能量耗散和振动抑制起关键作用，所提出的Fourier谱格式是一种高效、高精度的数值求解方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates a class of partially dissipative viscoelastic Timoshenko systems with memory, where dissipation is induced by a Volterra-type memory term acting only on the shear variable. The well-posedness of weak and strong solutions is established on finite time intervals, including existence, uniqueness, stability, and higher-order regularity under compatibility conditions consistent with mixed boundary conditions. For the numerical approximation, a Fourier spectral fully discrete scheme is constructed: sine and cosine basis expansions are used in space for unknowns satisfying Dirichlet and Neumann boundary conditions, respectively; in time, a central difference scheme is applied to the second-order derivatives, and the composite trapezoidal rule is used to approximate the memory convolution term. Based on a discrete energy method, the positivity of the constructed discrete energy is proved, and the error estimate for the fully discrete scheme with second-order convergence in time and \(q\)-th order in space is established for any q \in \mathbb{N}. Numerical experiments are given to verify the theoretical convergence rates and to compare the dynamic responses of the local and nonlocal models, demonstrating that the memory term effectively captures energy dissipation and vibration attenuation behavior in viscoelastic materials.

</details>


### [115] [Uniform-in-time Strong Error Estimates of Tamed-FEM to Superlinear SPDEs driven by Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09173)
*Jingjing Cai,Zhihui Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对一类由乘性噪声驱动的超线性随机偏微分方程（包括随机Allen-Cahn方程），建立了非线性显式驯服有限元方法的均匀时间强误差估计，证明了该方法的指数遍历性，并给出了精确不变测度与数值不变测度在Wasserstein-2距离下的收敛速率，最后通过数值实验验证了强收敛速率的尖锐性和时间无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为超线性SPDEs（如随机Allen-Cahn方程）设计一种能够长时间无条件稳定且保持Lyapunov结构的数值方法，并给出严格的误差分析和遍历性保证。

Method: 采用非线性显式驯服有限元方法（tamed FEM），该方法通过修改非线性项以避免数值爆破，实现长时间无条件稳定性，并保持原系统的Lyapunov结构。分析上建立了均匀时间强误差估计，并利用Wasserstein-2距离推导了不变测度的收敛率。

Result: 得到了尖锐且时间无关的强收敛速率，证明了该数值格式的指数遍历性，数值实验验证了理论结果的准确性。

Conclusion: 该驯服FEM能够有效模拟超线性SPDEs的长时间行为，具有均匀时间精度和遍历性保证，是此类问题可靠的长时程数值求解方案。

Abstract: We establish sharp, uniform-in-time strong error estimates for a nonlinearity-explicit tamed finite element method (FEM) applied to a class of superlinear stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) driven by multiplicative noise, including the stochastic Allen--Cahn equation with a moderately thick interface. This tamed-FEM was first introduced in [Z. Liu and J. Shen, arXiv:2502.19117] to ensure long-time unconditional stability and to preserve the Lyapunov structure of this class of SPDEs. We further prove that the scheme is exponentially ergodic and derive the convergence rate between the exact invariant measure and its numerical counterpart in the Wasserstein-2 distance. Finally, we present numerical experiments that verify the ergodicity as well as the sharpness and time-independence of the strong convergence rates for this tamed-FEM.

</details>


### [116] [An implicit octree-based adaptive Material Point Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09275)
*Robert E. Bird,William M. Coombs,Charles E. Augarde,Ted J. O'Hare*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文首次提出基于八叉树的隐式物质点法，通过自适应网格和材料点离散高效求解大变形连续体-结构接触问题，在HPC上实现最高5.5倍加速和21倍CO2减排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物质点法在处理连续体-结构大变形接触问题时，依赖结构化网格和高分辨率离散，导致计算系统庞大、运行时间长，尤其在三维修中更为突出。

Method: 提出基于八叉树背景网格的隐式物质点法，根据结构与连续体接触位置自动自适应调整计算网格和材料点离散，实现高效求解。

Result: 在多个大变形基准题和连续体-结构相互作用问题中，相比一致网格方法，在HPC上实现最高5.5倍加速和21倍CO2减排。

Conclusion: 八叉树隐式物质点法能高效求解大变形连续体-结构相互作用问题，显著提升计算效率并降低能耗。

Abstract: The Material Point Method provides an effective approach for modelling the large deformations that often arise from contact interactions between rigid structures and surrounding continua. However, solving these problems requires accurate representation of the continuum-structure interface, which necessitates high resolution background mesh and material point discretisations. This requirement, combined with evolving continuum-structure interfaces and the fact that most Material Point Method implementations are dependent on structured meshes, can result in large numerical systems and long run times especially when modelling problems in three-dimensions. Motivated by this issue, this paper provides the first octree-based implicit Material Point Method for efficient solution of large deformation continuum-structure interaction problems. The octree background mesh provides a natural way to automatically adapt both the computational mesh and the material point discretisation based on the position of the interaction between the structure and continuum. The new approach is demonstrated on a number of large deformation benchmark and continuum-structure interaction problems, where up to a 5.5-times speed up and a consequent 21-times CO2 saving is achieved when running on a HPC compared to results obtained using a conforming mesh.

</details>


### [117] [Justification and structure- and asymptotic-preserving discretizations of a hyperbolized Cahn-Hilliard equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09299)
*Jan Giesselmann,Fabio Leotta,Hendrik Ranocha,Jochen Schuetz*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Cahn-Hilliard（CH）方程的双曲近似（“双曲化”）及其在松弛极限下向CH模型的收敛性，发展了基于迎风格式和凸-凹分裂的IMEX能量稳定半离散格式，证明了该格式是渐近保持的，并通过相对能量框架指导参数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对Dhaouadi等人提出的CH方程双曲近似进行严格的数学收敛性分析，并发展数值方法使其在松弛极限下保持渐近相容性。

Method: 1) 通过形式渐近展开和相对能量框架证明双曲化模型在松弛极限下收敛于CH方程；2) 采用迎风求和分部（SBP）算子构造空间半离散格式；3) 结合凸-凹分裂的（加性）IMEX Runge-Kutta方法进行时间离散；4) 利用相对能量框架的先验误差估计指导参数选择。

Result: 所提出的数值方法在松弛参数趋于零时渐近保持，即收敛到原始CH方程的稳定离散格式。

Conclusion: 通过相对能量框架可以系统性地指导数值参数选择，保证格式的稳定性和渐近保持性。

Abstract: We study a hyperbolic approximation ("hyperbolization") of the Cahn-Hilliard (CH) equation, originally proposed by Dhaouadi, Dumbser, and Gavrilyuk (2025, DOI: 10.1098/rspa.2024.0606) and study its convergence towards the CH model in a relaxation limit both via formal asymptotic expansions and, for a slightly modified approximation, via the relative energy framework. Moreover, we develop energy-stable semidiscretizations of the CH equation and of this hyperbolization using upwind summation-by-parts operators in space. Subsequently, we combine them with (additive) implicit-explicit (IMEX) Runge-Kutta methods based on a convex-concave splitting. We show that the resulting method is asymptotic preserving, i.e., it converges in the limit of the relaxation parameter to a stable discretization of the original CH equation. The choice of the necessary parameters is guided by the a priori error estimate based on the relative energy framework.

</details>


### [118] [Residual Pseudospectra Reveal a Physics-Informed Koopman Backbone for Tropical Pacific Variability and ENSO Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09369)
*Paula Lorenzo-Sanchez,Matthew J. Colbrook,Antonio Navarra*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文通过结合核扩展动态模态分解、残差最小化和伪谱分析，将Koopman算子特征值关系作为物理一致性检验，从热带太平洋海表温度异常数据中识别出19个稳健的残差最小频率及其相干空间模态，构建了一个紧凑且可解释的Koopman骨干结构，该结构涵盖了从低频调制到准两年振荡的ENSO变率，并在8-18个月超前预报中展现出显著的技巧提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带太平洋SST变率涉及多尺度相互作用，ENSO是其年际主导表现，但组织这种变率并支撑扩展期可预报性的动力结构难以从高维观测中提取。Koopman算子学习能提供非线性动力学的谱坐标，但有限的地球物理记录往往产生密集、对采样敏感的频谱，其物理内容模糊不清。本文旨在揭示这种表观冗余背后的算子级相干结构，将动态一致性嵌入物理信息算子学习，将不透明频谱转化为稳健、可解释且具有预测能力的热带太平洋变率表示。

Method: 结合核扩展动态模态分解（kernel EDMD）与残差最小化和伪谱分析，将Koopman特征值关系作为物理一致性检验来组织学习到的频谱。应用于ERA5和HadISST热带太平洋SST异常数据，识别残差景观中的稳健残差最小频率及其相干空间模态。

Result: 识别出19个稳健的残差最小频率，具有跨产品和采样实现保持一致的相干空间模态。这些模态定义了一个紧凑的Koopman骨干，涵盖低频调制到准两年分量，包括ENSO频段变率。频谱云由该骨干的整数幂和非线性组合结构化，形成残差排序的Koopman层次结构。该骨干重建了显著的Nino3.4方差，并在样本外预测中展现出技巧，最大增益在8-18个月超前预报。

Conclusion: 通过将动态一致性嵌入物理信息算子学习，该框架将不透明频谱转化为稳健、可解释且具有预测能力的热带太平洋变率表示。识别出的Koopman骨干结构揭示了热带太平洋SST变率的多尺度组织机制，并为扩展期预测提供了物理基础。

Abstract: Tropical Pacific sea-surface-temperature (SST) variability spans interacting timescales, with the ENSO as its dominant interannual expression. Yet the dynamical structure organizing this variability and underpinning extended-range predictability remains difficult to extract from high-dimensional observations. Koopman operator learning offers spectral coordinates for nonlinear dynamics, yet finite geophysical records often produce dense, sampling-sensitive spectra whose physical content is ambiguous. We show that this apparent redundancy reflects coherent operator-level structure. Combining kernel Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition with residual minimization and pseudospectral analysis, we use the Koopman eigenvalue relation as a physics-informed consistency test to organize learned spectra. Applied to ERA5 and HadISST tropical Pacific SST anomalies, the residual landscape identifies 19 robust residual-minimum frequencies with coherent spatial modes that persist across products and sampling realizations. Together, these modes define a compact Koopman backbone spanning low-frequency modulation through quasi-biennial components, including ENSO-band variability. The surrounding spectral cloud is structured by integer powers and nonlinear combinations of this backbone, forming a residual-ordered Koopman hierarchy. The backbone reconstructs substantial Nino3.4 variance and enables skillful out-of-sample forecasts, with greatest gains at 8-18-month leads. By embedding dynamical consistency into physics-informed operator learning, the framework turns opaque spectra into robust, interpretable and predictive representations of tropical Pacific variability.

</details>


### [119] [Second-order bulk-surface splitting for the wave equation with kinetic boundary conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09502)
*R. Altmann,R. Morandin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对带动力学边界条件的半线性波动方程，提出了一种二阶体面分裂格式，通过将方程解释为耦合系统并针对离散状态位置实施不同差分格式，实现了体动力学与面动力学的解耦，在弱CFL条件下证明了能量稳定性和二阶收敛性，并进行了数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带动力学边界条件的半线性波动方程的高效数值求解问题，特别是实现体（bulk）与面（surface）动力学的解耦，以提高计算效率和精度。

Method: 将原方程重构为耦合系统，根据离散状态在系统方程中的具体位置实施不同的差分格式，构建了一个4步分裂方案，实现体动力学与面动力学的解耦。

Result: 在弱CFL条件下证明了该格式的能量稳定性和二阶收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的二阶体面分裂方案能够稳定、精确地求解带动力学边界条件的半线性波动方程，具备良好的理论保证和数值表现。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the numerical analysis of a second-order bulk--surface splitting scheme for the semi-linear wave equation with kinetic boundary conditions. The construction is based on the interpretation of the equations as coupled system and the implementation of different difference formulae for the discrete states depending on their exact position in the system equations. This results in a 4-step scheme which decouples bulk and surface dynamics. We prove energy stability and second-order convergence under a weak CFL condition and validate these results also numerically.

</details>


### [120] [Pressure-robust and quasioptimal Discontinuous Galerkin discretisations of the $p$-Stokes problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09586)
*P. A. Gazca-Orozco,M. Růžička*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有(p,δ)结构的非线性p-Stokes系统，提出了两种局部间断Galerkin（LDG）逼近方法。基于原始变分形式，在极小的正则性假设下证明了方法的适定性和稳定性（先验估计）。第一种方法具有压力鲁棒性和拟最优误差估计；第二种方法具有压力鲁棒性误差估计，并证明了对于所有p∈(1,∞)和δ≥0，线性基函数具有最优收敛性及收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有(p,δ)结构的非线性p-Stokes系统，目前缺乏在极小正则性假设下具有压力鲁棒性和最优收敛性的数值方法，因此需要设计并分析LDG逼近格式。

Method: 基于原始变分形式，设计了两种LDG方法：第一种方法满足压力鲁棒和拟最优误差估计；第二种方法在更一般的条件下（所有p∈(1,∞)，δ≥0）证明了压力鲁棒误差估计和最优收敛性（线性基函数）。

Result: 在极小正则性假设下证明了两种LDG方法的适定性和稳定性；第一种方法获得了压力鲁棒和拟最优误差估计；第二种方法对所有p∈(1,∞)和δ≥0实现了压力鲁棒的最优收敛性（线性基函数）。

Conclusion: 提出的两种LDG方法能有效逼近具有(p,δ)结构的非线性p-Stokes系统，在极小正则性条件下具有压力鲁棒性和最优/拟最优收敛性，为非线性流体问题的数值求解提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In the present paper, we propose Local Discontinuous Galerkin (LDG) approximations for a nonlinear system of $p$-Stokes type, having $(p,δ)$-structure. On the basis of the primal formulation, we prove well-posedness and stability (a priori estimates) of the methods under truly minimal regularity assumptions. We show that the first method possesses a pressure-robust and quasi-optimal error estimate, and discuss its consequences. Moreover, we propose a second method, for which we show a pressure-robust error estimate and prove convergence and convergence rates, which are optimal for linear ansatz functions for all $p\in (1,\infty)$ and $δ\geq 0$.

</details>


### [121] [A Differentiable Simulation of the Eye for Patient-Specific Strabismus Surgery Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09661)
*Even Harsigny,Pablo Alvarez,Michel Duprez,Stéphane Cotin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理建模的个性化斜视手术规划方法，通过构建可微分的眼部物理仿真器，结合患者特异性几何结构和Hill-type肌肉生物力学模型，优化手术剂量以最小化角度偏差。在正视眼模型上验证与标准手术表高度吻合，而对于高度近视等非典型解剖结构患者，模型计算出临床显著增加的手术剂量需求，为个性化斜视手术提供了更精准的规划方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统斜视手术依赖医生经验和统计参考表，对于眼形态异常的患者常无法达到最佳矫正效果。本研究旨在开发一种基于物理建模的个性化手术规划方法，以考虑患者特异性眼部解剖结构，提高手术剂量准确性。

Method: 构建了包含患者特异性几何结构和Hill-type肌肉生物力学的眼部物理仿真器，通过求解优化问题找到最小化角度偏差的手术剂量，模型实现为完全可微分的仿真，支持高效优化。在正视眼上验证后应用于解剖学异常的虚拟患者。

Result: 正视眼模型预测与标准手术表高度吻合；对于高度近视模型，框架计算出比标准眼显著增加的手术剂量需求，该后徙差异以0.5 mm为调整增量在临床上具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 该模型能够提供校准的手术方案，与标准表不同，它还考虑了涉及非典型眼形态的病理情况。这一患者特异性模型是实现个性化手术规划的一步，有望提高非典型病例的手术剂量准确性和手术效果。

Abstract: Purpose: Up to 4% of adults will develop strabismus in their lifetime. The most common surgical intervention involves adjusting the length of one or more extraocular muscles to correct the angular deviation. This correction depends on surgical expertise and statistical reference tables, which often fail to yield optimal results for patients with atypical eye morphology. Our work proposes a physics-based modeling approach to personalized surgical planning, accounting for patient-specific eye anatomy. Methods: We built a physics-based simulator of the eye and its muscles, incorporating patient-specific geometry and Hill-type muscle biomechanics. We solve an optimization problem to find the surgical dosage that minimizes angular deviation. The model is implemented as a fully differentiable simulation, enabling efficient optimization. We validated the framework by comparing its predictions with standard surgical tables for emmetropic eyes before applying it to anatomically atypical virtual patients. Results: Our model's predictions for emmetropic eyes were first validated, demonstrating a strong fit with standard surgical tables. More importantly, for high-myopia models, the framework computed a clinically significant increase in the required surgical dosage compared to standard eyes. This computed recession difference is highly relevant as surgical plans are adjusted in 0.5 mm increments. Conclusion: Our results show that our model provides a calibrated surgical plan that, unlike standard tables, also accounts for pathologies involving atypical eye shapes. This patient-specific model represents a step toward personalized surgical planning, with the potential to improve dosage accuracy and surgical outcomes for atypical cases.

</details>


### [122] [Boundary-Layer-Induced Failure of Standard Physics-Informed Neural Networks: A Legendre Wavelet Collocation Benchmark for Singularly Perturbed Transport Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09676)
*Suvendu Nayak,Arun Kumar Gupta*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文对比了局部Legendre小波配点法(LWM)与标准软边界物理信息神经网络(PINN)在求解一维奇异摄动对流边界层问题上的表现。结果表明，LWM在Peclet数高达1000时仍能保持误差低于5×10⁻³，而标准PINN在强边界层情况下失效，证明局部小波配点法更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边界层问题在数值求解中具有挑战性，因为解在大部分区域几乎不变而在边界附近剧烈变化。本文旨在比较局部Legendre小波配点法与物理信息神经网络在处理此类问题时的可靠性和精度。

Method: 采用局部Legendre小波配点法，使用局部支撑的Legendre多项式基函数，将问题转化为包含残差方程、边界方程和界面连续性方程的代数配点系统。与标准软边界物理信息神经网络进行对比实验，设置Peclet数为1、10、100和1000。

Result: LWM在所有四种Peclet数下均能捕捉边界层，最大误差低于5×10⁻³。标准软边界PINN在低Peclet数时表现良好，但在高Peclet数时无法解析锐利边界层。在边界附近密集评估有助于揭示粗网格上可能遗漏的误差。

Conclusion: 对于该基准问题，局部小波配点法比标准软边界PINN更可靠。

Abstract: Boundary layers provide a demanding test for numerical solvers because the solution may remain almost constant over most of the domain while changing rapidly in a narrow region near the boundary. This paper studies a singularly perturbed one-dimensional transport boundary-value problem with increasing Peclet number $(\mathrm{Pe})$. A local Legendre wavelet collocation method (LWM) is compared with a standard soft-boundary physics-informed neural network (PINN) for this benchmark. The wavelet approximation uses locally supported Legendre polynomial basis functions and converts the problem into a square algebraic collocation system with residual, boundary, and interface-continuity equations. Numerical experiments are performed for $\mathrm{Pe}=1,10,100,$ and $1000$. The LWM captures all four cases, with the largest error remaining below $5\times 10^{-3}$. The standard soft-boundary PINN performs well for the mild cases but fails to resolve the sharp boundary layer for the larger Peclet numbers. The results show that local wavelet collocation is more reliable than the standard soft-boundary PINN for this benchmark, while dense near-boundary evaluation helps reveal errors that may be missed on coarse grids.

</details>


### [123] [A space-time sparse-grid method for the wave equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09688)
*Matteo Ferrari,Andrea Moiola,Chiara Perinati,Ilaria Perugia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对线性波动方程开发了一种快速时空数值格式，采用基于稀疏网格组合技术的强制时空离散化方法，实现了高效并行化，并提供了严格的收敛性和计算复杂度理论分析，数值实验验证了方法的效率和理论估计的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种能够高效并行化且具有严格理论保证的快速时空数值格式，用于求解线性波动方程。

Method: 采用稀疏网格组合技术应用于强制时空离散化，设计基于张量积时空离散化的方法，实现求解器的并行化。

Result: 建立了收敛速率和计算复杂度的理论估计，数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性和方法的效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的时空数值格式在理论上具有严格的收敛性和复杂度保证，在实践中具有良好的并行效率和计算性能。

Abstract: We develop a fast space-time numerical scheme for approximating solutions to the linear wave equation. The approach is based on the sparse-grid combination technique applied to a coercive space-time discretization. Designed for tensor-product space-time discretizations, the method enables efficient parallelization of the resulting solver. We provide a rigorous theoretical analysis establishing convergence rates and computational complexity estimates. Numerical experiments validate the theoretical estimates and demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed method.

</details>


### [124] [Low-Rank Acceleration of the Operator Fourier Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09689)
*Jack Kelley*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合算子傅里叶变换（OFT）和低秩交叉近似（Cross-DEIM）的数值算法，用于高效求解二维结构化网格上的亥姆霍兹方程。通过将问题分解为薛定谔方程在伪时间上的积分，并利用低秩方法降低计算成本，在特定问题类别上实现了显著的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亥姆霍兹方程的高效数值求解在计算科学中具有重要价值，但传统OFT方法的主要计算瓶颈在于求解薛定谔方程，尤其在高维度或高网格分辨率下成本高昂。为此，作者希望引入低秩方法来克服这一计算负担，并突破维数灾难的限制。

Method: 采用算子傅里叶变换（OFT）框架将亥姆霍兹方程转化为两个近轴算子的乘积形式，进而分解为薛定谔方程在伪时间上的积分；同时引入低秩交叉近似方案（Cross-DEIM），利用解中存在的低秩结构来加速薛定谔方程的求解过程。

Result: OFT与低秩Cross-DEIM方法的组合在特定问题类别上实现了大幅度的计算成本降低，验证了该方法在高维或高分辨率场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 结合OFT与低秩近似方法能够有效提升亥姆霍兹方程的求解效率，尤其在问题中存在低秩结构时，该方法能够显著降低计算成本，具有一定的普适性和推广价值。

Abstract: We develop a numerical algorithm for the efficient solution or approximation of solutions to the Helmholtz equation on a structured grid in two dimensions. We make use of the Operator Fourier Transform (OFT) and a low-rank cross approximation scheme (Cross-DEIM) to decompose the problem into an integral over a pseudo-time of solutions to the Schrödinger equation. The OFT is a framework for solving operator equations like fractional Laplacian equations or the Helmholtz equation, when the latter is written as a product of two paraxial operators. The main computational cost in the OFT is the solution to the Schrödinger equation, especially when the dimension or mesh resolution is high. In this work, we alleviate this cost by utilizing a low-rank method. Such methods aim to beat the curse of dimensionality when low-rank structures are present in the solution. We show that the combination of these two approaches can have large cost reductions for certain classes of problems.

</details>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [VoLo: A Physical Orchestrator for Open-Vocabulary Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07723)
*Siyi Chen,Hugo Hadfield,Alex Zook,Mikaela Angelina Uy,Chan Hee Song,Erwin Coumans,Xuning Yang,Faisal Ladhak,Qing Qu,Stan Birchfield,Jonathan Tremblay,Valts Blukis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VoLoAgent，一个由VLM驱动的闭环代理系统，通过将VLA/WAM等机器人能力作为可中断工具进行编排，实现开放词汇的长时域操作，并引入RoboVoLo基准评估该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇的长时域操作需要机器人能理解灵活指令、处理复杂多物体场景，并自适应地规划、执行、监控和从失败中恢复。现有方法缺乏对物理世界中决策时机、动作时序和工具调用的有效编排。

Method: 提出VoLoAgent，构建闭环代理系统：VLM作为核心编排器，将VLA/WAM作为可中断工具进行"中程引导"（mid-rollout steering），同时结合视觉模型和动作原语，实现规划、监控与恢复的完整循环。

Result: 在RoboVoLo基准上，VoLoAgent显著优于单一VLA/VLM或基于工具的系统，并在真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 物理编排（Physical Orchestration）是关键，VLM对异构机器人能力的动态编排和中断控制能有效应对开放词汇长时域操作的挑战。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary long-horizon manipulation requires robots to reason over flexible instructions and complex multi-object scenes while adaptively planning, executing, monitoring, and recovering from failures. We address these demands with a closed agent loop in which a VLM orchestrates heterogeneous robot capabilities as interruptible tools. Unlike in virtual AI agents, the timing of decisions, actions and tool calls is important in a physical world that does not pause for reasoning. We refer to this setting as Physical Orchestration, and propose VoLoAgent, a VLM that plans, monitors, and recovers by treating a VLA/WAM as an interruptible tool it steers mid-rollout alongside vision models and action primitives. To evaluate these long-horizon capabilities, we introduce RoboVoLo, a high-fidelity benchmark for open-vocabulary long-horizon manipulation across common sense, memory/state tracking, complex references, and world knowledge, with both task-level success and failure-mode diagnostics. Experiments show VoLoAgent substantially outperforms single VLA/VLM or tool-based systems, with validation on real-robot experiments. Project page: https://chicychen.github.io/VoLo/

</details>


### [126] [MinNav: Minimalist Navigation Using Optical Flow For Active Tiny Aerial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07813)
*Aniket Patil,Mandeep Singh,Uday Girish Maradana,Nitin J. Sanket*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MinNav，一种基于光流及其不确定性的导航系统，使微型无人机能够在无需先验知识的情况下，通过在场景中探索性运动来避开静态和动态障碍物并穿越未知形状的间隙，在真实实验中达到70%的成功率，是首个仅用单目相机解决上述全部导航问题的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型空中机器人依赖单目相机实现自主导航，现有方法在处理静态/动态障碍物和未知形状间隙时往往需要深度信息或先验知识，计算开销大，难以在计算资源有限的微型无人机上实时运行。

Method: 提出MinNav导航栈，核心基于光流及其不确定性估计，利用机器人的主动探索行为在场景中移动以发现障碍物并规划导航路径，无需任何场景组件的先验知识或位置/顺序信息。

Result: 在多种真实环境（含静态/动态障碍物和未知形状间隙）中成功验证，整体成功率达70%，计算量比基于深度的方法低数个数量级，性能与深度方法相当，可在微型无人机上实时运行。

Conclusion: MinNav是首个仅用单目相机且无需先验知识就能同时处理静态/动态障碍物和未知形状间隙导航的方案，在计算效率上具有显著优势，适合部署于微型空中机器人。

Abstract: Navigation using a monocular camera is pivotal for autonomous operation on tiny aerial robots due to their perfect balance of versatility, cost and accuracy. In this paper, we introduce MinNav, a navigation stack based on optical flow and its uncertainty to fly through a scene with static and dynamic obstacles and unknown-shaped gaps without any prior knowledge of the scene components and/or their locations/ordering. We further improve success rate by using the activeness of the robot to move around in an exploratory way to find obstacles and navigate. We successfully evaluate and demonstrate the proposed approach in many real-world experiments in various environments with static and dynamic obstacles and unknown-shaped gaps with an overall success rate of 70%. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first solution to tackle all the aforementioned navigation cases without prior knowledge using a monocular camera. Our approach is on par in performance with depth based methods with factors of magnitude less computation required and can readily run onboard tiny aerial robots. The accompanying video, supplementary material, code and dataset can be found at https://pear.wpi.edu/research/minnav.html

</details>


### [127] [Path Planning Using Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07855)
*Qiang Le,Yaguang Yang,Isaac E. Weintraub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）的自主车辆路径规划方法，将威胁建模为圆形禁区，通过强化学习实现实时安全导航，其奖励函数包含目的地吸引场、障碍物排斥场和控制能耗惩罚。与传统伪谱最优控制方法相比，该方法在保证路径有效性的同时显著提升了计算速度，并能够预先评估任务可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 威胁环境下自主车辆的路径规划问题本质是非线性非凸的，传统最优控制方法计算时间过长，无法满足实时决策需求，亟需一种快速有效的路径规划方法。

Method: 采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）算法，将威胁建模为多个圆形禁区；奖励函数由三部分组成：目的地吸引场、障碍物排斥场和控制能量消耗惩罚（偏好直线行驶）；通过模拟环境中的试错学习，训练智能体从当前状态（位置和航向）到可行动作的直接映射。

Result: 仿真验证表明，DDPG方法能够生成有效路径，且计算速度显著快于传统伪谱最优控制方法，更适合实时应用；同时能够找出保证安全到达目的地的最大起始点集合，为任务规划提供可行性预判。

Conclusion: 基于学习的DDPG方法在威胁环境路径规划中兼顾了路径有效性和计算效率，为实时任务规划提供了可行方案，尤其适用于需要快速决策的自主导航场景。

Abstract: Path-planning for autonomous vehicles in threat-laden environments is a fundamental challenge because the problem is nonlinear and nonconvex even in simplest scenarios. While traditional optimal control methods can be used to find ideal paths, the computational time is often too slow for real-time decision-making. To solve this challenge, we propose a method based on Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) and model the threat as possibly multiple circular 'no-go' zones. A mission is regarded as a failure if the vehicle enters this restricted zone at any time or does not reach a neighborhood of the destination. The DDPG agent is trained through trial and error in a simulated environment, learning a direct mapping from its current state (position and heading) to a series of feasible actions that guide the agent to safely reach its destination. The reword function has three parts: (a) an attractive field centered at the final destination, (b) some repulsive fields centered at the origins of circular obstacles, and (c) a penalty of control energy consumption (the magnitude of heading change) that indirectly in favor for straight path. The DDPG trains the agent using these incentives to find the largest possible set of starting points wherein a safe path to the destination is guaranteed. This provides critical information for mission planning, showing beforehand whether a task is achievable from a given starting point, assisting pre-mission planning activities. The approach is validated in simulation. A comparison between the DDPG method and a traditional optimal control (pseudo-spectral) method is carried out. The results show that the learning-based agent produces effective paths while being significantly faster, making it a better fit for real-time applications.

</details>


### [128] [End-to-End Control of a Powered Knee-Ankle Prosthesis Towards Unified, Tuning-Free Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07902)
*John Shim,Christoph Nuesslein,Sixu Zhou,Hanjun kim,Kinsey Herrin,Aaron Young*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的假肢控制器，利用时间卷积网络从机载传感器直接估计连续致动器信号，无需意图分类器和个体调参。在18名经股截肢者数据集上训练后，成功实现在五种步态模式下的实时部署。实验表明，该控制器能再现训练数据中的踝关节力矩-速度缩放关系、斜坡适应以及无缝楼梯转换，为无需个体调参的统一模式自适应假肢辅助提供了初步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
