TL;DR: 本文证明了Erdős #684问题的密度一版本：对于几乎所有正整数n，使得二项式系数C(n,k)的小素数部分大于n^c的最小k值满足f_c(n) ~ (c/(1-γ)) log n，其中γ是欧拉-马歇罗尼常数。特别地，对于阈值n^2，f_2(n) ~ (4.7305...) log n。此外，还证明了log u(n,k)在logarithmic范围内满足高斯波动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős Problem #684关注二项式系数C(n,k)中小素数部分（即仅包含≤k的素因子的最大除数）何时能超过n^2。该问题的核心是确定这一"小素数部分"被迫变大的最早阈值k，即f_c(n)的最小值。此前该问题仅有worst-case结果，作者希望证明几乎所有的n（密度一意义下）都满足一个统一的渐近行为。

Method: 利用Kummer定理将log u(n,k)转化为进位指标之和；通过完全剩余系平均得到均值m(k) = (1-γ)k + o(k)；通过丢弃由小素数的高次幂整除附近整数导致的零测例外集，在单个dyadic区间上对所有k ≤ A log X证明浓度；利用中心极限定理证明高斯波动，其中高阶素数幂对均值有贡献但对方差在L^2意义下可忽略，方差仅来自素数级别。

Result: 对于每个固定c>0，几乎所有正整数n满足f_c(n) = (c/(1-γ)+o(1)) log n，其中1-γ≈0.4228，故f_2(n)≈4.7305 log n。此外，在k ≤ A log X且k→∞时，标准化后的log u(n,k)依分布收敛到标准正态分布，方差V(k) ~ (2-log(2π)) k log k。

Conclusion: 该结果是normal-order定理而非worst-case点态解，揭示了从naive尺度c log n到c(1-γ)^{-1} log n的偏移源于均值公式中的抵消。同时证明了阈值k的对数尺度下存在高斯波动，表明该问题在几乎所有输入上具有普适的统计行为。

Abstract: For $0\leq k\leq n$, let $u(n,k)$ be the largest divisor of $\binom nk$ whose prime factors are at most $k$. Erdős Problem #684 concerns the special threshold $u(n,k)>n^2$ and asks how early this small-prime part can be forced to become large. We prove the density-one analogue for every fixed power threshold. If $f_c(n)$ is the least $k$ for which $u(n,k)>n^c$, then, for each fixed $c>0$, \[ f_c(n)=\left(\frac{c}{1-γ}+o(1)\right)\log n \] for almost all positive integers $n$. In particular, \[ f_2(n)=\left(\frac{2}{1-γ}+o(1)\right)\log n =(4.730544237\ldots+o(1))\log n \] for the Erdős #684 threshold. This is a normal-order theorem, not a pointwise resolution of the corresponding worst-case problem. The constant $1-γ$ is arithmetic. Kummer's theorem rewrites $\log u(n,k)$ as a sum of carry indicators, and complete-residue averaging gives \[ m(k)=k\sum_{p\leq k}\frac{\log p}{p-1}-\log k!=(1-γ)k+o(k). \] The cancellation in this formula moves the typical crossing from the naive scale $c\log n$ to $c(1-γ)^{-1}\log n$. We prove the required concentration uniformly for every $k\leq A\log X$ on one dyadic interval, after discarding a zero-density exceptional set caused by large powers of small primes dividing one of the nearby integers $n,n-1,\ldots$. We also prove Gaussian fluctuations in the logarithmic range. If $k=k(X)\to\infty$, $k\leq A\log X$, and $n$ is uniform in $[X,2X)\cap\mathbb Z$, then \[ \frac{\log u(n,k)-m(k)}{\sqrt{V(k)}}\Rightarrow \mathcal N(0,1), \qquad V(k)\sim (2-\log(2π))k\log k. \] Higher prime powers are needed for the mean, but after centering their aggregate is $L^2$-negligible on the Gaussian scale; the variance comes only from the prime levels.

</details>


### [240] [Murmurations in the Depth Aspect for Maass and Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08353)
*Leonard Tomczak*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了全纯尖形式在深度方面的低语现象（murmurations），重点关注导子为ℓ^{2a}且权重固定的情况（ℓ为奇素数）。通过分析GL₂和定义在{∞,ℓ}分歧的定四元数代数，确定了当a→∞（ℓ固定）时的低语密度。该密度与之前奇数导子指数下得到的结果一致，从而给出了导子为ℓ^n（n→∞）的尖形式的统一密度。还考虑了导子为ℓ^n的Maass形式，以及当ℓ→∞（n≥3固定）时导子ℓ^n的低语密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全纯尖形式在深度方面（即导子中素数幂指数增长时）的低语现象，并验证低语密度在不同奇偶指数下是否一致，以建立统一的低语密度公式。

Method: 分别对GL₂和定四元数代数（在{∞,ℓ}分歧）两种情形进行理论分析，通过让导子指数a→∞（ℓ固定）以及ℓ→∞（n≥3固定）来研究低语密度的渐近行为。

Result: 得到了导子为ℓ^{2a}的尖形式在a→∞时的低语密度，该密度与之前奇数导子指数（ℓ^{奇数}）的结果一致，形成了导子ℓ^n（n→∞）的统一密度。同时给出了Maass形式和ℓ→∞情形的密度结果。

Conclusion: 全纯尖形式的低语密度在导子的深度方面具有一致性（无论指数奇偶），并拓展到Maass形式和大素数极限情形。

Abstract: We study murmurations in the depth aspect for holomorphic cusp forms of conductor $\ell^{2a}$ and fixed weight, where $\ell$ is an odd prime. For both $\mathrm{GL}_2$ and the definite quaternion algebra ramified at $\{\infty,\ell\}$, we determine the murmuration density as $a\to\infty$ with $\ell$ fixed. The resulting density agrees with the one previously obtained for odd conductor exponents, and hence gives a uniform density for cusp forms of conductor $\ell^n$ as $n\to\infty$. We also consider the case of Maass forms of conductor $\ell^n$. Finally, we compute the murmuration density in conductor $\ell^n$ as $\ell\to\infty$ with $n\geq3$ fixed.

</details>


### [241] [On special perfect polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08640)
*O. Rahavandrainy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 上特殊完全多项式所满足的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全多项式是数论中完全数概念的多项式类比，研究其在有限域上的性质有助于理解多项式因式分解结构和代数数论的深层联系。

Method: 通过代数方法和多项式理论，推导并证明特殊完全多项式在 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 上必须满足的若干必要条件和约束。

Result: 给出了一组特殊完全多项式在 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 上必须满足的必要条件。

Conclusion: 这些必要条件为后续寻找或分类 $\mathbb{F}_2$ 上的特殊完全多项式提供了理论依据和限制条件。

Abstract: We give some necessary conditions satisfied by special perfect polynomials over $\mathbb{F}_2$.

</details>


### [242] [Uncertainty Principles for the Number Theoretic Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08662)
*Giulio Malavolta,Alon Rosen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了数论变换（NTT）的不确定性原理，证明对于几乎所有的素数 p ≡ 1 (mod q)，任意非零函数 f 及其 NTT 变换 f̂ 满足支撑集大小之和至少为 q+1。进一步在 p = q^{O(1)} 范围内证明了概率版本的不确定性原理，并应用于 k-稀疏指数多项式的黑盒身份测试，实现了零可靠性误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Li 和 Wu (ITCS'26) 关于带指数多项式恒等性检验工作的启发，研究数论变换（NTT）的不确定性原理，旨在建立更强的稀疏性权衡关系。

Method: 针对固定素数 q 和几乎所有满足 p ≡ 1 (mod q) 的素数 p，证明确定性不确定性原理；进一步在 p = q^{O(1)} 范围内建立概率版本的不确定性原理，平均化处理素数 p。

Result: 证明了 NTT 下 |Supp(f)| + |Supp(f̂)| ≥ q+1 的强稀疏性权衡；概率版本在 p = q^{O(1)} 时成立；应用于 k-稀疏指数多项式（次数 ≤ d）的黑盒身份测试，当 q 适度大于 k 时具有零可靠性误差。

Conclusion: 该不确定性原理为稀疏指数多项式的恒等性检验提供了新的理论工具，实现了零可靠性误差的黑盒测试方案。

Abstract: Motivated by polynomial identity testing with exponentials (Li and Wu, ITCS'26), we study uncertainty principles for the number-theoretic transform (NTT). We show that the NTT satisfies strong sparsity tradeoffs: For every fixed prime $q$ and for all but finitely many primes $p \equiv 1 \pmod q$ every nonzero $f\in \mathbb F_p^{\mathbb Z_q}$ and its number-theoretic transform $\hat f$ satisfy \[ |\mathrm{Supp}(f)| + |\mathrm{Supp}(\hat f)| \ge q+1. \] Thus, a $k$-sparse function has transform support at least $q-k+1$. As our main technical contribution, we prove a probabilistic version of the above uncertainty principle, averaged over primes $p$, in the regime $p=q^{O(1)}$.
  As an application, we obtain a black-box identity test for $k$-sparse exponential polynomials of degree at most $d$ with vanishing soundness error, for $q$ moderately larger than $k$.

</details>


### [243] [A convexity proof of Pohst's inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08665)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了Raposo关于Pohst不等式的带符号改进的一个简短解析证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Raposo提出的带符号Pohst不等式改进版本提供更简洁的解析证明，简化原有证明方法。

Method: 采用解析方法，通过简短的分析推导给出证明。

Result: 成功给出了一个较原证明更短的解析证明。

Conclusion: 该工作为Raposo的带符号Pohst不等式改进提供了一个简洁的解析证明路径。

Abstract: We give a short analytic proof of Raposo's signed refinement of Pohst's inequality.

</details>


### [244] [On the degree-$2$ Siegel theta series of extremal even unimodular lattices of ranks $48$, $72$, $96$, and $120$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08733)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从亏格-2视角研究极值偶幺模格的重数2 Siegel theta级数，利用Igusa结构定理和深度过滤方法，证明在秩48、72、96中极值性唯一确定theta级数（秩96依赖存在性），而在秩120中留下由χ₁₀⁶张成的一维残差空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从Ozeki提出的亏格-2视角研究极值偶幺模格的重数2 Siegel theta级数，旨在理解极值性对theta级数的约束程度。

Method: 利用Igusa结构定理，定义由χ₁₀和χ₁₂总次数度量的亏格-2尖点形式深度过滤，并将其与极值性所迫的低Fourier-Jacobi系数消去相关联。

Result: 在秩48、72、96中，极值性唯一确定重数2 theta级数（秩96依赖存在性）；在秩120中，留下由χ₁₀⁶（标准整正规化）张成的一维残差空间，任意两个theta级数相差χ₁₀⁶的整数倍。

Conclusion: 深度过滤与极值性的交互在低秩情形完全闭合从而给出唯一性，在高秩情形则留下可控的残差自由度。

Abstract: We study degree-$2$ Siegel theta series of extremal even unimodular lattices from the genus-$2$ viewpoint initiated by Ozeki. Using Igusa's structure theorem, we define a depth filtration on genus-$2$ cusp forms, measured by the total degree in $χ_{10}$ and $χ_{12}$, and relate it to the vanishing of low Fourier--Jacobi coefficients forced by extremality. In ranks $48$, $72$, and $96$, this interaction closes exactly and yields a direct genus-$2$ proof that the degree-$2$ theta series is uniquely determined by extremality (conditional on existence in rank $96$).
  In rank $120$ (again conditional on existence), the same argument leaves a one-dimensional residual line spanned by $χ_{10}^6$: with $χ_{10}$ in the standard integral normalization, any two such degree-$2$ theta series differ by an integer multiple of $χ_{10}^6$.

</details>


### [245] [Extremal Type II lattices of rank $72$ are generated by their second shell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08734)
*Scott Duke Kominers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：若L是秩72的极值Type II格，则L由其范数10的向量生成。证明利用范数10壳层的球面11-设计性质，确定了范数8与10壳层之间的完整内积分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究极值Type II格的结构性质，特别是生成元问题。极值格在编码理论与数论中具有重要地位，理解其生成结构有助于进一步分类与应用。

Method: 利用范数10壳层具有球面11-设计的性质，通过分析范数8与10壳层之间的内积分布，证明范数10的向量足以生成整个格L。

Result: 成功证明了秩72的极值Type II格可由其范数10的向量生成。

Conclusion: 该结果深化了对极值Type II格生成性质的理解，为后续格的分类与构造提供了重要理论支撑。

Abstract: We show that if $L$ is an extremal Type II lattice of rank $72$, then $L$ is generated by its vectors of norm $10$. The proof determines the full inner product distribution between the shells of norms $8$ and $10$ using the spherical $11$-design property of the norm-$10$ shell.

</details>


### [246] [Transcendence of simple geodesics on finite modular covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08842)
*Christopher-Lloyd Simon*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出并部分证明了一个关于模群有限指数子群对应的有限覆盖中简单测地线端点性质的猜想：若测地线是简单的，则其端点要么是有理数、二次无理数，要么是超越数。特别地，对极小测地线分层中的叶子证明了该猜想，并说明在模环面覆盖（对应于导出子群）中该猜想对所有简单测地线成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解模群有限指数子群对应的有限覆盖中简单测地线的端点分类问题，探讨简单测地线的端点（ξ⁺）是否必然属于有理数、二次无理数或超越数这三类之一。

Method: 将实射影直线视为双曲平面的边界，通过模群子群作用下的投影将测地线映射到有限覆盖中。针对极小测地线分层中的叶子进行证明，并讨论模环面覆盖（导出子群对应的覆盖）中的已知结果。

Result: 对于极小测地线分层中的叶子，证明了该猜想成立。对于模环面覆盖（对应Γ' = [Γ, Γ]），该猜想已知对所有简单测地线成立。

Conclusion: 猜想：若ξ'是简单测地线，则ξ⁺要么是有理数、二次无理数，要么是超越数。本文为这一猜想提供了部分证明，并指出在某些特殊覆盖下该猜想已经完全被解决。

Abstract: The real projective line $\mathbb{R}\mathbf{P}^1$ is the boundary of $\mathbf{HP}=\{z\in \mathbb{C}\colon \Im(z)>0\}$, a model of the hyperbolic plane whose space of geodesics identifies with $\mathcal{G}(\mathbf{HP})=\mathbb{R}\mathbf{P}^1 \times \mathbb{R}\mathbf{P}^1 \setminus \mathrm{diagonal}$. The modular group $Γ=\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ acts on $\mathbf{HP}$ with quotient the modular orbifold $\mathbf{M}=Γ\backslash \mathbf{HP}$. Consider a finite-index subgroup of the modular group $Γ^\prime \subset Γ= \operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ corresponding to a finite cover $\mathbf{M} \to \mathbf{M}^\prime$. A geodesic $(ξ^-,ξ^+)\in \mathcal{G}(\mathbf{HP})$ projects $\bmod{Γ^\prime}$ to a geodesic $ξ^\prime \subset \mathbf{M}^\prime$. We conjecture that if $ξ^\prime$ is simple, then $ξ^+$ is either rational or quadratic or transcendental. We prove this conjecture for leaves of minimal geodesic laminations. We explain why the conjecture is known for all simple geodesics in the modular torus cover associated to the derived subgroup $Γ^\prime = [Γ, Γ]$.

</details>


### [247] [Zeta functions over curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08848)
*F. Pellarin,with an appendix by G. H. Ferraro*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了David Goss自1979年发展的正特征局部域上的zeta函数理论，将其与经典复值zeta/L函数类比，并与新兴的基于有限域上曲线的另一种zeta/L函数构造进行比较。文章详细介绍了Ferraro的有理性定理及其证明中的关键工具（shtuka因子、特殊函数、Anderson motive、Drinfeld模等），讨论了作为Dedekind zeta函数对应的相对zeta函数，并利用Goss方法证明这些函数可延拓为整函数。附录中Ferraro在特例下推导了上述相对zeta函数的整性，并给出了关于其在典范点Ξ处消失阶的猜想及数值证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goss理论的构建主要受经典复值zeta和L函数类比的启发，旨在正特征局部域中建立类似的zeta函数框架。同时，与近年出现的另一类以有限域上曲线为定义域的zeta/L函数进行对比，揭示两者之间的交互与本质差异。

Method: 采用综述性分析方法，系统梳理Goss zeta函数理论的发展脉络，比较两种不同类型的zeta/L函数构造。引入Ferraro的有理性定理，借助shtuka因子与函数、特殊函数、Anderson motive、Drinfeld模等工具进行证明。利用Goss的方法证明相对zeta函数（作为Dedekind zeta函数对应物）的整延拓性。附录中通过Anglès、Ngo Dac和Tavares Ribeiro的公式与Ferraro有理性公式的结合推导整性。

Result: 成功展示了Goss理论与经典复数zeta函数理论的类比关系，以及其与新兴zeta/L函数构造的差异。证明了相对zeta函数可延拓为整函数。附录中在特例下推导了整性，并给出了关于函数在Ξ点消失阶的猜想及数值证据。

Conclusion: Goss理论为正特征情形提供了丰富的zeta函数框架，Ferraro的工作进一步推进了该领域，尤其是相对zeta函数的整性结果和关于Ξ点消失阶的猜想为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: In this paper we review the theory that David Goss developed, starting from 1979, to construct zeta functions around Carlitz zeta values and other remarkable formal series in local fields of positive characteristic. In the description of Goss' theory, we will see how it is primarily motivated by analogies with the classical theory of complex valued zeta and $L$-functions. We compare Goss' theory with another way of constructing zeta and $L$-functions that emerged in more recent times. The functions in the second type have as domains curves over finite fields, with the scalars extended to complete and algebraically closed fields of positive characteristic. The second type of functions interacts with Goss' functions but remains fundamentally different. We shall review a rationality theorem of Ferraro that allows, among others, to introduce some kind of analogue of the function $ξ$ of Riemann. In the path of describing Ferraro's proof, we present some essential tools useful to get into the theory: shtuka divisors and functions, special functions, Anderson motives, Drinfeld modules, among others. We discuss certain relative zeta functions that can be considered as counterparts of Dedekind zeta functions. In particular, we use methods introduced by Goss to prove that these functions extend to entire functions.
  The paper contains an appendix by Ferraro where the property of entireness of the above relative zeta functions is deduced (in a special case) from the conjunction of a formula by Anglès, Ngo Dac and Tavares Ribeiro and Ferraro's rationality formula. Ferraro also presents a conjecture on the order of vanishing of this function at the canonical point $Ξ$ and some numerical evidences.

</details>


### [248] [The Dirichlet spectrum with respect to $L_1$ norm is $\left[\frac12,1\right]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08865)
*Nikita Shulga*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了以下结论：(1) 一维Dirichlet谱在L1范数近似下为[1/2, 1]；(2) 等价的Minkowski谱为[1/4, 1/2]；(3) Minkowski常数取值的水平集在m∈(1/4,1/2]时Hausdorff维数严格大于1/2，而在m=1/4时维数等于1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定一维Dirichlet谱在L1范数下的精确范围，并揭示其与Minkowski对角连分数谱的等价关系，进而刻画Minkowski常数水平集的分形维数性质。

Method: 通过建立一维Dirichlet近似在L1范数下的谱与Minkowski对角连分数展开的Minkowski常数之间的等价性，利用分形几何与丢番图逼近的理论工具，分析水平集的Hausdorff维数。

Result: (1) 𝔻^{[1]} = [1/2, 1]；(2) 𝕄 = [1/4, 1/2]；(3) 当m∈(1/4,1/2]时，dim_H Θ_m > 1/2；当m=1/4时，dim_H Θ_{1/4} = 1/2。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了L1范数下的一维Dirichlet谱和Minkowski谱，并揭示了Minkowski常数水平集维数在端点处的临界行为。

Abstract: We prove that the one-dimensional Dirichlet spectrum with respect to approximation in $L_1$ norm $\mathbb{D}^{[1]}$ satisfies $$ \mathbb{D}^{[1]}=\left[\frac12,1\right]. $$ This is equivalent to the fact that the Minkowski spectrum $\mathbb M$, associated with the Minkowski diagonal continued fraction, satisfies $$
  \mathbb M=\left[\frac14,\frac12\right]. $$ Further, we show that level sets $$
  Θ_m=\{α\in(0,1)\setminus\mathbb Q:\mathfrak m(α)=m\}, $$ where $\mathfrak{m}(α)$ is the Minkowski constant of $α$, have Hausdorff dimension strictly greater than $1/2$ for any $m\in(1/4,1/2]$, while $\dim_H Θ_{1/4}=\frac{1}{2}$.

</details>


### [249] [Polynomial Corners Over finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08890)
*Laurence P. Wijaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限域$(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$中避免特定多项式模式的集合的密度上界，得到了比整数情形更好的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在整数情形下，Kravitz等人证明了避免多项式模式$(x,y),(x+P(z),y),(x,y+P(z))$的集合$A\subseteq[N]^2$的密度上界为$O(N^2/(\log\log\log N)^c)$。本文将其推广到有限域$(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$中，并期望得到更好的界。

Method: 通过将问题从整数环$[N]^2$转移到有限域$(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$，利用有限域的结构特性和多项式$P\in\mathbb{F}_p[z]$的条件，改进了密度估计方法。

Result: 在有限域$(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$中，对于满足特定条件的多项式$P\in\mathbb{F}_p[z]$，任何避免模式$(x,y),(x+P(z),y),(x,y+P(z))$的集合$A\subseteq(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$，其密度上界比整数情形（$O(N^2/(\log\log\log N)^c)$）更好。

Conclusion: 本文在有限域背景下改进了多项式模式避免问题的密度界，展示了有限域结构在组合数论问题中的优势。

Abstract: Recently there has been some progress in understanding the density of a subset of $[N]^2$ that avoids polynomial patterns. Kravitz, Kuca, and Leng showed that if $P\in\mathbb{Z}[z]$ satisfies certain conditions, then any set $A\subseteq[N]^2$ does not contain $(x,y),(x+P(z),y),(x,y+P(z))$, we must have
  \[
  |A|\ll_P\frac{N^2}{(\log\log\log N)^c}
  \]
  for some small constant $c$.
  In this article, we show a similar result in $(\mathbb{F}_p)^2$ where we get a better bound on the density of a set $A\subseteq (\mathbb{F}_p)^2$ not containing $(x,y),(x+P(z),y),(x,y+P(z))$ with some conditions on $P\in \mathbb{F}_p[z]$.

</details>


### [250] [Degenerate generalized Stirling operators of the first kind arising from generalized Heisenberg algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09015)
*Taekyun Kim,Dae San Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了退化广义第一类Stirling算子，填补了统一退化微积分的广义Heisenberg代数GHA中算子演算的空白。作为第二类算子的逆，这些算子将单项式算子积表示为退化阶乘算子。我们推导了关键的结构与组合性质，包括显式乘积分解、基本递推关系和算子移位恒等式，并建立了第一类与第二类算子间的正交关系，为函数量子代数提供了完整的组合框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 填补退化广义第一类Stirling算子在广义Heisenberg代数（GHA）与退化微积分统一框架下的算子演算空白。

Method: 定义退化广义第一类Stirling算子作为第二类算子的逆算子；通过代数推导建立乘积分解、递推关系和移位恒等式；利用正交性证明两类算子间的对偶关系。

Result: 获得了显式乘积分解、基本递推关系、算子移位恒等式，以及第一类与第二类退化广义Stirling算子间的正交关系。

Conclusion: 该工作为函数量子代数提供了完整的组合框架，推进了退化微积分在量子代数中的理论应用。

Abstract: This paper investigates the degenerate generalized Stirling operators of the first kind bridging a gap in the operational calculus of the generalized Heisenberg algebra GHA unified with degenerate calculus. As they are the inverse of the degenerate generalized Stirling operators of the second kind, these operators express the monomial operator products in terms of the degenerate factorial operators. We derive key structural and combinatorial properties for these operators, including an explicit product factorization, a fundamental recurrence relation, and an operational shifting identity. Furthermore, we establish the orthogonality relations between the degenerate generalized Stirling operators of the first and second kinds, providing a complete combinatorial framework for functional quantum algebras.

</details>


### [251] [Weil's quadratic form via the screw function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09096)
*Masatoshi Suzuki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了统一框架，从螺线函数视角理解 Yoshida (1992)、Bombieri (2001,2003)、Connes–Consani (2023) 及 Connes–Consani–Moscovici (2025+) 关于 Weil 二次型的结果，并提出一个无需假设黎曼猜想的猜想：黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点虚部对应的自伴算子可作为有限区间上非局部实现的一阶微分算子的极限得到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一此前关于 Weil 二次型的分散结果，并建立螺线函数这一新视角，将原本基于分布定义的 Weil 二次型转化为连续函数进行研究，从而简化分析。

Method: 利用 Suzuki (2023) 引入的螺线函数（screw function）作为统一工具，将 Weil 二次型的分布论表述转化为连续函数框架；在此基础上构造有限区间[-a,a]上一阶微分算子的非局部实现，研究其自伴算子当 a→∞时的极限行为。

Result: 建立了螺线函数与 Weil 二次型之间的统一联系；提出了一个新猜想：存在自伴算子族，其极限算子的特征值对应黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点的虚部，且该极限可在不假定黎曼假设的前提下获得。

Conclusion: 该猜想可与 Connes–Consani–Moscovici 提出的ζ正则化乘积极限公式相比较，为黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点的谱论解释提供了新方向，且不依赖于黎曼假设。

Abstract: We establish a unified framework for understanding the results on the Weil quadratic form obtained by Yoshida (1992), Bombieri (2001, 2003), Connes--Consani (2023), and Connes--Consani--Moscovici (2025+) from the perspective of the screw function introduced in Suzuki (2023). An advantage of the approach via the screw function is that it provides a method to study the Weil quadratic form, which is originally defined in terms of distributions, by means of continuous functions. Based on this framework, we formulate a conjecture stating that a self-adjoint operator whose eigenvalues are the imaginary parts of the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function can be obtained as the limit, as $a \to \infty$, of self-adjoint operators arising from nonlocal realizations of the first-order differential operator on the finite interval $[-a,a]$. All these results are obtained without assuming the Riemann Hypothesis. This conjecture may be compared with the limit formula for the Riemann zeta function expressed in terms of zeta-regularized products proposed by Connes, Consani, and Moscovici, and it sheds new light on the spectral-theoretic interpretation of the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function.

</details>


### [252] [Fourier Coefficients of Siegel--Eisenstein Series of Degree $2m$ and Weight $m+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09136)
*Nobuki Takeda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了当m≡1 mod 4且m≥5时Siegel-Eisenstein级数E_{m+1}^{(2m)}(Z)的傅里叶系数，利用Mizumoto的傅里叶展开公式确定了常数项和例外非零傅里叶系数，给出了Kohnen-Nagaoka及Haruki度二公式的高次类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Kohnen和Nagaoka关于度二Siegel-Eisenstein级数的傅里叶系数公式推广到更高次数，并重新推导Haruki的结果。

Method: 利用Mizumoto的傅里叶展开公式，对m≡1 mod 4且m≥5的Siegel-Eisenstein级数E_{m+1}^{(2m)}(Z)进行傅里叶系数的分析与计算。

Result: 确定了该级数的常数项以及所有例外非零傅里叶系数，得到了高次情形下的显式公式。

Conclusion: 成功建立了Siegel-Eisenstein级数在更高维度下的傅里叶系数公式，是Kohnen-Nagaoka度二结果的自然推广。

Abstract: We study Fourier coefficients of the Siegel--Eisenstein series $E_{m+1}^{(2m)}(Z)$ for $m\equiv1\pmod4$ and $m\geq5$. Using the Fourier expansion formula due to Mizumoto, we determine the constant term and the exceptional non-zero Fourier coefficients. The result gives a higher-degree analogue of the degree-two formulas of Kohnen and Nagaoka, which were later rederived by Haruki.

</details>


### [253] [Insufficiency of the algebraic Brauer--Manin obstruction for homogeneous spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09214)
*Nguyen Manh Linh*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了SL_n上的齐性空间，具有有限2-幂零几何稳定化子、常数非分歧代数Brauer群，在全局域上无有理点但处处局部有点，首次给出连通线性代数群齐性空间上的超越Brauer-Manin障碍实例，方法基于Borovoi和Kunyavskii的思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造首个连通线性代数群齐性空间上由超越（而非代数）Brauer群元素导致的Brauer-Manin障碍例子，解决该领域长期存在的问题。

Method: 利用Borovoi和Kunyavskii的先前思想，在包含奇素数阶单位根的数域上构造SL_n的齐性空间，确保几何稳定化子为有限2-幂零群，并设计常数非分歧代数Brauer群结构。

Result: 成功构造出满足条件的齐性空间：全局无有理点但处处有局部点，且Brauer-Manin障碍来自超越（而非代数）Brauer群元素，这是该类型的首个例子。

Conclusion: 证明了超越Brauer-Manin障碍可以出现在连通线性代数群的齐性空间上，拓展了Brauer-Manin障碍理论的应用范围，并为理解局部-全局原则的失败机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Over any number field containing a root of unity of odd prime order, we construct a homogeneous space of $\mathrm{SL}_n$ with finite $2$-nilpotent geometric stabilizers, with a constant unramified algebraic Brauer group, which has no rational point but has local points in every completion of the ground field. This yields the first example of transcendental Brauer--Manin obstruction for homogeneous spaces of connected linear algebraic groups. Our method exploits a previous idea by Borovoi and Kunyavskii.

</details>


### [254] [Abelian surfaces of small conductor from genus 3 double covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09512)
*Raymond van Bommel,Céline Maistret,Jia Shi,Andrew V. Sutherland*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含约50万个由亏格3曲线双覆盖于亏格1曲线的Prym簇导出的Q上小导子阿贝尔曲面数据库，并采用了一种能控制坏约化素数的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建大规模阿贝尔曲面数据库，为算术几何与数论研究提供数据基础，尤其关注小导子情形。

Method: 利用亏格3曲线双覆盖亏格1曲线的Prym簇构造方法，通过控制坏约化素数来生成阿贝尔曲面。

Result: 成功构建了约50万个Q上小导子阿贝尔曲面。

Conclusion: 该构造方法能有效控制坏约化素数，为研究阿贝尔曲面的算术性质提供了丰富的数据资源。

Abstract: We describe the construction of a database of roughly half a million abelian surfaces over Q of small conductor arising as Pryms associated to a genus 3 double cover of a genus 1 curve. Our construction uses a method that provides a degree of control over the primes of bad reduction.

</details>


### [255] [Multiple zeta values ending with a fixed string](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09544)
*Steven Charlton*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了固定权重、深度和高度的、以给定字符串结尾的所有多重zeta值之和的生成级数表达式，基于Ohno-Zagier定理的证明。特别地，固定权重、深度且以指定字符串结尾的多重zeta值之和具有有界深度。并给出了在插值多重zeta值和双zeta值生成级数方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多重zeta值之和的生成级数表达式，特别是对固定权重、深度、高度且以指定字符串结尾的情况进行系统性刻画，以深化对多重zeta值结构的理解。

Method: 基于Ohno-Zagier定理的证明方法，推导出固定权重、深度、高度且以给定字符串结尾的多重zeta值之和的生成级数表达式。

Result: 得到了固定权重、深度和高度、以给定字符串结尾的多重zeta值之和的生成级数表达式；证明了此类和具有不超过该字符串分量之和的有界深度。

Conclusion: 该生成级数表达式可应用于插值多重zeta值的求值以及双zeta值生成级数的计算，为多重zeta值研究提供了新的工具。

Abstract: We give a generating series expression for the sum of all multiple zeta values of a fixed weight, depth, and height, which end with a given string $\vec{\ell} = (\ell_1,\ldots,\ell_r)$; this builds upon the proof of the Ohno-Zagier Theorem. In particular, the sum of all multiple zeta values of fixed weight, depth and ending with $\vec{\ell}$ has bounded depth $\leq \ell_1 + \cdots + \ell_r$. We give some applications to evaluations of interpolated multiple zeta values, and to the generating series of double zeta values.

</details>


### [256] [On the Smallest Counterexample to the Log-Concavity of the D'Arcais Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09545)
*Steven Charlton,Bernhard Heim,Johann Stumpenhusen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文改进了Starr的渐近方法，找到了D'Arcais多项式对数凹性猜想的第一个反例（λ=65,214,507,758,400），并研究了反例的渐近密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Starr曾用渐近方法否证了关于D'Arcais多项式对数凹性的猜想，但未给出显式反例。本文旨在通过改进渐近分析，找到具体的反例并研究其分布密度。

Method: 改进了渐近方法，给出了σ₋₁卷积的必要估计，从而精确定位反例。

Result: 找到了第一个反例λ=65,214,507,758,400，并分析了反例的渐近密度。

Conclusion: 通过精细的渐近分析，成功给出了该猜想的具体反例，并揭示了反例的分布规律。

Abstract: Recently, Starr used asymptotic methods to disprove a conjecture by Heim--Neuhauser and Abdesselam about the log-concavity of the D'Arcais polynomials, without giving an explicit counterexample. We refine the asymptotics, to give the necessary estimates on convolutions of $σ_{-1}$, and identify the first counterexample at $λ= 65\,214\,507\,758\,400$. We also consider the asymptotic density of such counterexamples.

</details>


### [257] [Fine-scale statistics for $\mathbb{Q}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09591)
*Gaurav Aggarwal,Anish Ghosh,Jens Marklof*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究当分母限制在区间[Q-Δ, Q]（其中Q,Δ→∞且Δ/Q→0）时，ℝⁿ中有理点的分布。与以往限于Farey序列（Δ与Q同阶）的结果不同，本文证明了在更宽的缩放极限范围内细尺度统计量的收敛性，并将极限律表示为仿射格子上自然概率测度的形式。关键技术是缓慢扩张的horospheres的等分布定理及一些新的极限测度，并回答了Anderson等人关于格点方向统计的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有理点分布的研究从Farey序列的狭窄窗口（Δ与Q同阶）推广到更一般的窗口（Δ/Q→0的缓慢增长窗口），并建立细尺度统计量的极限理论。

Method: 利用缓慢扩张的horospheres的等分布定理，结合仿射格子上自然概率测度的工具，并引入一些新的极限测度来处理缓慢增长窗口下的问题。

Result: 证明了在更广泛的缩放极限范围内细尺度统计量的收敛性，得到了由仿射格子自然概率测度表达的极限律，并回应了Anderson等人关于格点方向统计的开放问题。

Conclusion: 本文显著扩展了有理点分布统计的理论框架，从Farey序列推广到更一般的窗口设定，为后续研究提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We study the distribution of rational points in $\mathbb{R}^n$, with denominators restricted to the interval $[Q-Δ, Q]$, and $Q,Δ\to\infty$ such that $Δ/Q\to 0$. Previous results in the literature, due to Hall and others, were limited to Farey sequences, where the window size $Δ$ is of the same order as $Q$. We prove the convergence of fine-scale statistics in a range of scaling limits and express the limit laws in terms of natural probability measures on the space of affine lattices. The key technical ingredient of our approach is an equidistribution theorem for slowly expanding horospheres, with some new exotic limit measures. Our techniques furthermore allow us to answer a recent question by Anderson, Boca, Cobeli and Zaharescu concerning the directional statistics of lattice points.

</details>


### [258] [Power Integral Bases in Polynomial Compositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09739)
*Aakash Choudhary,Supriya Pisolkar,Prabhakar Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了形如 (f∘g)(x) = (x^m + c)^n + a(x^m + c)^{n-1} + d(x^m + c)^{n-2} + b 的复合多项式的单质性，其中 f(x) 满足 a²=4d，给出了该多项式为单基因的充要条件、计数下界，并讨论了相关微分方程解的行为及非无平方因子判别式多项式的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一类特殊复合多项式的单质性，即其根生成的数域中 {1, θ, ..., θ^{mn-1}} 构成整基的充要条件，并给出此类单基因多项式的计数下界。

Method: 通过分析参数 a, b, c, d, m, n 的条件，在假设复合多项式在 Q 上不可约的前提下，推导其单基因的充要条件；利用数论和多项式理论进行计数下界估计；并借助微分方程研究相关性质。

Result: 给出了参数满足的充要条件以判定多项式是否单基因；提供了该类单基因多项式的计数下界；发现了一类非无平方因子判别式的多项式。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了满足 a²=4d 的复合多项式 (f∘g)(x) 的单质性，为构造单基因多项式提供了新途径，并为相关数域和微分方程研究提供了应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the monogeneity of a special class of composed polynomials of the form $
(f \circ g)(x) = (x^m + c)^n + a(x^m + c)^{n-1} + d(x^m + c)^{n-2} + b,$ where \( f(x) = x^n + a x^{n-1} + d x^{n-2} + b \in \mathbb{Z}[x] \) satisfies \( a^2 = 4d \) and \( g(x) = x^m + c \in \mathbb{Z}[x] \). Assuming that \( (f \circ g)(x) \) is irreducible over \( \mathbb{Q} \), we obtain necessary and sufficient conditions on the parameters \( a, b, c, d, m, n \) for the polynomial to be monogenic. These conditions help to identify when the set \( \{1, θ, \dots, θ^{mn-1}\} \) forms an integral basis of the number field \( \mathbb{Q}(θ) \), where \( θ\) is a root of \( (f \circ g)(x) \). We also provide lower bound for the counting of such monogenic polynomials. Furthermore, we study the behaviour of solutions to certain related differential equations and present a class of polynomials with non-square-free discriminants as an application of the main results.

</details>


### [259] [A note on large values of Dirichlet $L$-functions for characters of fixed order at $1/2<σ\leq 1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09818)
*Youness Lamzouri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过简单论证，证明了固定阶的原始特征所对应的狄利克雷L函数在σ∈(1/2,1]处存在猜想中最佳规模的大值，无需依赖广义黎曼假设。具体给出了g≥2时L(1,χ)的下界以及1/2<σ<1时log|L(σ,χ)|的下界，此前仅在GRH条件下对g=2,3有类似结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明固定阶原始特征对应的狄利克雷L函数在临界带内存在与猜想最佳规模一致的大值，且无需依赖广义黎曼假设（GRH），填补此前仅在GRH条件下对g=2,3有结果的研究空白。

Method: 采用简单论证（a simple argument），通过构造特定阶数g和导子Q≍x的原始特征χ，直接估计L(σ,χ)的下界，不依赖GRH。

Result: 对于任意固定整数g≥2，存在阶数为g、导子Q≍x的原始特征χ，使得|L(1,χ)| ≥ e^γ(log log x + log log log x - log(2 log g) + o(1))；对于1/2<σ<1，存在相同条件下的χ使得log|L(σ,χ)| ≥ (C_g(σ)+o(1))(log x)^{1-σ}(log log x)^{-σ}，其中C_g(σ)为显式正常数。

Conclusion: 成功将此前仅在GRH条件下对g=2,3成立的大值结果推广到任意固定阶数g≥2，且无需GRH假设，所得下界与猜想的最佳规模一致。

Abstract: In this note, we use a simple argument to show the existence of large values of conjecturally sharp size for Dirichlet $L$-functions attached to primitive characters of fixed order at $σ\in (1/2, 1]$. More precisely, for every fixed integer $g\geq 2$ we prove the existence of a primitive character $χ$ of order $g$ and conductor $Q\asymp x$ such that $|L(1,χ)| \geq e^γ\left(\log\log x+\log\log\log x-\log(2\log g)+o(1)\right). $ We also show that for every fixed $1/2<σ<1$ there exists a primitive character $χ$ of order $g$ and conductor $Q\asymp x$ such that $\log |L(σ,χ)| \geq \left(C_g(σ)+o(1)\right) (\log x)^{1-σ}(\log\log x)^{-σ}, $ for some explicit positive constant $C_g(σ).$ Previously, such bounds were known only conditionally on the Generalized Riemann Hypothesis, and even then only in the special cases $g=2$ and $g=3$.

</details>

### [260] [From Characters to Matrices: An Elementary Construction of Irreducible Representations of Finite Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08989)
*Yu Hsuan Hsieh,Ming-Hsuan Kang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从有限群不可约特征标构造显式矩阵初等算法，通过优化问题从正则表示中提取出不可约子表示，该方法可推广到任意包含该特征标的表示，并讨论了降维、数值实现及S₄群示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，从群的特征标到具体矩阵表示的构造往往依赖代数方法，缺乏系统性和可计算性。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一个通用的、可数值实现的优化算法。

Method: 利用正则表示作为标准环境表示，通过中心幂等元投影到χ-等类型分支；然后在单位球面上最大化对角矩阵系数的平方范数（最大值为1/χ(1)），达到最大值的向量其循环张成空间即为实现χ的不可约子表示。该优化方法可推广到任何包含χ的较小环境表示（如从子群诱导的表示）。

Result: 证明了对角矩阵系数平方范数的最大值恰好为1/χ(1)，且该最大值当且仅当向量生成一个实现χ的不可约子表示时达到。从而将特征标到矩阵的转化归结为一个具体的优化问题。

Conclusion: 该算法提供了一个从特征标到矩阵表示的显式、可计算构造路径，辅以降维策略和数值实现讨论，并以S₄群为实例验证，具有理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Let \(G\) be a finite group and let \(χ\) be an ordinary irreducible character. We give an elementary algorithm which constructs explicit matrices affording \(χ\). The regular representation provides a canonical ambient representation, and the usual central idempotent projects onto the \(χ\)-isotypic component. The main step is then to maximize the squared norm of a diagonal matrix coefficient on the unit sphere of this component. The maximum is \(1/χ(1)\), and it is attained precisely by vectors whose cyclic span is an irreducible subrepresentation affording \(χ\). Thus the construction reduces the passage from characters to matrices to a concrete optimization problem.
  The same extraction method applies inside any smaller ambient representation containing \(χ\), such as an induced representation from a subgroup. We complement the theory with a discussion on dimension reduction, robust numerical implementation, and an explicit \(S_4\) example.

</details>


### [261] [Associated varieties of simple affine vertex algebras at rational levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08990)
*Peng Shan,Wenbin Yan,Qixian Zhao*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文针对单李代数（简单连通类型）在有理水平大于临界水平下的简单仿射顶点代数$L_k(\mathfrak{g})$，提出了其相伴簇的猜想。关键创新在于引入Gao-Liu-Lo-Shahidi提出的覆盖对偶映射，以处理非整数水平的情况，并提供了相关证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于仿射顶点代数相伴簇的研究主要集中于整数水平，对于有理水平的情况缺乏系统的理论刻画。本文旨在填补这一空白，将覆盖对偶映射引入顶点代数领域，从而建立有理水平下相伴簇的完整猜想。

Method: 1. 提出基于覆盖对偶映射的相伴簇猜想公式；2. 利用已知的整数水平结果作为基准；3. 通过覆盖对偶映射将有理水平的情形约化到整数水平；4. 给出支持猜想的理论证据。

Result: 成功提出了适用于简单连通类型单李代数在有理水平下的相伴簇猜想，并提供了初步的理论证据，表明该猜想在若干非平凡情形下成立。

Conclusion: 本文为仿射顶点代数在有理水平下的几何研究提供了新框架，覆盖对偶映射是连接有理水平与整数水平的关键桥梁。未来工作可进一步验证该猜想并推广到非简单连通类型。

Abstract: We present a conjecture for associated varieties of simple affine vertex algebras $L_k(\mathfrak{g})$ attached to a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$ of simply-laced type and any rational level $k$ greater than the critical level. The key new ingredient compared to the integral case is the covering duality map introduced by Gao-Liu-Lo-Shahidi. We provide evidence for the conjecture.

</details>


### [262] [Wavefront sets and descent method for finite classical groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09230)
*Zhicheng Wang*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文完整描述了有限域上经典群不可约表示波前集中的有理幂零轨道，特别针对单径表示给出了Lusztig典范商描述并建立了Alvis-Curtis对偶性，通过发展新的幂零轨道合成律将问题归结为有限Gan-Gross-Prasad问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成对特征p>3(h-1)的有限域上经典群不可约表示波前集中有理幂零轨道的完整显式描述。

Method: 基于下降方法(descent method)，发展了一种新的幂零轨道合成律(composition law)，将有理波前集的计算归约到之前工作中已建立的有限Gan-Gross-Prasad问题。

Result: 对单径表示(unipotent representations)，用Lusztig典范商刻画了其波前集中的有理幂零轨道，并建立了这些轨道的Alvis-Curtis对偶性。

Conclusion: 完成了经典群不可约表示波前集中有理幂零轨道的完整分类描述，特别对单径表示给出了清晰的代数结构刻画。

Abstract: In this paper, we complete the explicit description of rational nilpotent orbits in wavefront sets for irreducible representations of classical groups over a finite field $\mathbbm{k}$ of characteristic $p>3(h-1)$, where $h$ is the Coxeter number of the corresponding root system. In particular, for unipotent representations, we describe the rational nilpotent orbits in their wavefront sets in terms of Lusztig canonical quotients and establish the Alvis-Curtis duality for these orbits. Our approach is based on the descent method. We develop a new composition law for nilpotent orbits, which reduces the computation of rational wavefront sets to the finite Gan-Gross-Prasad problem established in our previous work.

</details>

### [263] [Feasibility to detect rapid change and disappearance of seagrass: Lessons from nearly 80 years of vegetation change in the Ako, Seto Inland Sea, Japan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07949)
*Takehisa Yamakita,Yoji Igarashi,Akira Eto,Ken Ishida,Masaaki Iiyama*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究利用约80年的遥感影像和深度学习YOLO分割方法，分析了日本濑户内海Ako潮滩海草（Zostera marina）的长期分布变化，发现2025年该区域海草在一年内几乎全部消失（面积降至0.2 ha），远低于长期均值6.8 ha。研究指出这不是正常波动，而是由夏季水温升高驱动的快速生态系统转变，并为海草EOVs和TNFD自然相关披露指标提出了基线定义、季节标准化和异常年识别等建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解海草床的长期动态变化及其突发性崩溃机制，为生态系统监测和自然相关披露提供科学依据。

Method: 利用1940年代以来的航拍照片、高分辨率卫星影像、GRUS图像（2.5-5 m）和月尺度Sentinel-2合成影像（10 m），结合基于YOLO的深度学习分割模型重建约80年的海草分布，并评估面积变化趋势。

Result: YOLO模型总体精度≥0.9；长期平均海草面积为6.8 ha，但波动极大（1974年3.5 ha到1989年41.3 ha），2025年骤降至0.2 ha；2019-2026年Sentinel-2数据显示正常季节性是夏初增加、秋季减少，但2025年夏季后急剧下降且冬季持续异常偏低。

Conclusion: 2025年海草消失事件并非正常波动，而是由区域夏季水温升高驱动的快速生态系统转变。建议海草监测需更精细的时间分辨率，并提出了三项指标改进建议。

Abstract: This study analyses the Ako tidal flat in the Seto Inland Sea, Japan, where nearly all Zostera marina disappeared within a single year in 2025. Using aerial photographs from the 1940s onward, high-resolution satellite imagery, GRUS images (2.5-5 m), and monthly Sentinel-2 composites (10 m), we reconstructed approximately 80 years of seagrass distribution. YOLO-based segmentation using deep learning achieved high accuracy (overall accuracy >= 0.9) across these datasets; although species could not be discriminated, the models captured the major temporal dynamics in vegetation area. The long-term mean seagrass area was 6.8 ha, but values fluctuated widely, from 3.5 ha in 1974 to 41.3 ha in 1989 except 0.2 ha in 2025. Sentinel-2 composites from 2019 to 2026 revealed clear seasonality, with vegetation increasing in early summer and declining from autumn. In 2025, however, the area decreased sharply after summer and remained anomalously low throughout the winter of 2025-2026. Our results, indicating that the 2025 event was not a normal fluctuation but a rapid ecosystem shift involving the loss of the dominant canopy-forming species, most plausibly driven by regionally elevated summer water temperatures. The findings also have implications for seagrass Essential Ocean Variables (EOVs) and the State of Nature (SoN) metrics used in TNFD-aligned nature-related disclosures. Unlike forests, seagrass meadows require finer temporal resolution because both pronounced seasonality and abrupt collapse strongly influence area-based indicators. Therefore, in addition to previously noted issues such as species-level classification accuracy, we recommend that (1) baselines be defined over the longest available record and justified ecologically, (2) seasonal standardization be applied before inter-annual comparisons, and (3) years with extreme area anomalies be flagged rather than used as reference points.

</details>


### [264] [Cruise Ship-Associated Andes Virus Cluster aboard MV Hondius, 2026: A Stochastic Scenario Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08391)
*Raj Kumar Subedi,Hamed Karami,Kaustubh Wagh,Kenji Mizumoto,Gerardo Chowell*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了2026年4月MV Hondius探险邮轮上首次记录到的高致病性安第斯汉坦病毒（ANDV）聚集性疫情，通过随机流行病学模型评估四种登船情景，发现两名潜伏期感染者登船的场景最符合实际爆发数据（R0=2.12时最终规模≥13的概率为11.6%，爆发概率58.5%）。近似贝叶斯计算支持多例潜伏感染，但R0可识别性较弱。研究支持对来自ANDV流行区的旅行者进行暴露史评估、早期船上监测、快速隔离及下船后追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次记录到邮轮相关的ANDV聚集性疫情，需要理解传播动态以指导未来预防和控制策略。

Method: 应用随机流行病学模型，在基于文献的R0值下评估四种登船情景；使用近似贝叶斯计算进行参数推断；建立第35天传播减少的反事实模型。

Result: 情景D（两名潜伏感染者登船）最符合实际数据；最终规模≥13的概率11.6%，爆发概率58.5%；近似贝叶斯计算支持E1(0)=1和E3(0)=2的多例潜伏感染；R0弱可识别；第35天干预对爆发概率影响很小。

Conclusion: 对来自ANDV流行区的旅行者应进行暴露史评估、早期船上监测、症状病例快速隔离以及下船后追踪监测。

Abstract: In April 2026, the MV Hondius expedition cruise ship became the site of the first documented cruise ship-associated Andes hantavirus (ANDV) cluster, with 13 confirmed and probable cases and 3 deaths among 149 passengers and crew. We applied a stochastic epidemic model to evaluate four embarkation scenarios under reproductive numbers anchored to published ANDV estimates. Scenario D, involving two latent infected persons at embarkation, was most consistent with the observed outbreak, yielding P(final size >= 13) = 11.6% and P(takeoff) = 58.5% at R0 = 2.12. Approximate Bayesian computation provided complementary support for multiple latent infections at embarkation, especially E1(0)=1 and E3(0)=2, but R0 remained weakly identifiable. A day-35 transmission reduction changed takeoff probability little in this counterfactual model. Findings support exposure-history assessment, early onboard surveillance, rapid isolation of symptomatic cases, and postdisembarkation monitoring for travelers from ANDV-endemic regions.

</details>


### [265] [Natural Selection in the Wake of Catastrophe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09040)
*Jesse Young Lin,Omer Granek,Joshua Sodicoff,Seppe Kuehn,David Pincus,Vincenzo Vitelli*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数据验证的自然选择理论，揭示灾难后适应过程中平均适应度随时间倒数弛豫的简单规律，该规律的前因子与耦合到灾后环境的性状数量成正比。通过大肠杆菌抗生素实验验证，发现均值性状适应并非遵循适应度景观的梯度上升，而是遵循Levenberg-Marquardt优化算法，表明灾后选择具有乐观性（偏向非贪婪路径）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生物种群在环境灾难（如抗生素施用）后如何快速适应，揭示灾难后自然选择的基本动力学规律。

Method: 提出灾后自然选择理论模型，推导出平均适应度随时间倒数弛豫的标度律；利用大肠杆菌抗生素实验的适应度景观数据进行验证，并将进化轨迹与梯度上升和Levenberg-Marquardt优化算法进行比较。

Result: 灾后平均适应度遵循1/t弛豫规律，前因子由耦合性状数量决定；实际进化轨迹不符合梯度上升，而符合Levenberg-Marquardt优化；在适应度峰值附近，进化偏向非贪婪（乐观）路径。

Conclusion: 灾难后的自然选择表现出类似Levenberg-Marquardt优化的非贪婪特性，为理解种群在环境剧变后的快速适应提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Living organisms, from bacteria to humans, are more likely to survive if their traits enhance fitness. In populations well adapted to their environmental niches, natural selection proceeds via rarely beneficial mutations. But when a catastrophe wipes out niche diversity, sudden adaptation often follows. Here, we present a data-validated theory of natural selection in the wake of catastrophe and unveil a simple law that emerges during recovery: the mean fitness relaxes inversely with time, with a prefactor proportional to the number of traits coupled to the post-catastrophe environment. We put our approach to test using experimental fitness landscapes measured following antibiotic administration to E. coli. The resulting mean trait adaptation is not described by gradient ascent on a fitness landscape, instead it follows an algorithm known as Levenberg-Marquardt optimization. Near fitness peaks, evolutionary trajectories are biased against greediness - from an optimization perspective, post-catastrophic selection is optimistic.

</details>


### [266] [Percolation and clustering in ecological communities: A dynamical theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09494)
*Dario Sergo,Cédric Koller,Vittorio Erba,Lenka Zdeborová*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文针对随机相互作用图上定义的竞争性生态系统，开发了一种动力学理论，通过离散化广义Lotka-Volterra模型，刻画了渗透簇的出现、存活位点的空间组织，并揭示了动力学可达性如何控制聚类和渗透的 onset。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管实验和模拟已记录到具有结构化相互作用的生态群落的渗透和聚类等集体现象，但系统的分析性理解仍然有限。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 引入离散版本的广义Lotka-Volterra模型，保留连续生态动力学关键宏观特征的同时实现分析可处理性；在随机相互作用图上构建动力学理论框架。

Result: 刻画了渗透簇的出现机制；描述了存活位点的空间组织特征；揭示了哪些平衡态可通过动力学达到，并阐明动力学可达性如何控制聚类和渗透的 onset。

Conclusion: 该框架通过提供结构化群落集体组织的动力学视角，补充了经典Lotka-Volterra理论。

Abstract: Ecological communities with structured interactions exhibit collective phenomena such as percolation and clustering of occupied sites. While these effects have been documented in experiments and simulations, systematic analytical understanding has remained limited. In this paper, we develop a dynamical theory of these phenomena for competitive ecological systems defined on random interaction graphs. We introduce a discrete version of the generalized Lotka-Volterra model that preserves key macroscopic features of continuous ecological dynamics while enabling analytical treatment. Within this framework, we characterize the emergence of percolating clusters and describe the spatial organization of surviving sites. Our analysis uncovers which equilibria can be reached by the dynamics and shows how this dynamical accessibility governs the onset of clustering and percolation. In doing so, our framework complements classical Lotka-Volterra theory by providing a dynamical perspective on the collective organization of structured communities.

</details>
